ākiyāl, iṟivaṉṟāṉ, palavēṟu vakippaṭa aḷaviṉṟi virinta poruḷkaḷiṉ ūṭē yellām ūṭuruvi niṉṟu iṭiyaṟātu periyatōr aruṭṭoḻil purikiṉṟa vaṉākak kāṇappaṭutaliṉ, avaṉuṭiya kaṭikkaṇ nōkkat tiṟku oru ciṟiyēṉākiya yāṉ eṅṅaṉam poruḷāvēṉ eṉṟu eḻuvatāṉa tuyara niṉivi muḻutum oḻittuviṭak kaṭavē ṉāka; ēṉeṉil, ellā viṭaṅkaḷilum ūṭuruviyiruntu ellām aṟiya vallavaṉāṉa avaṉatu pērāṟṟalukkum perun taṉmikkum yām avvāṟu eṇṇutal ōr iḻukkamākaliṉ eṉka.
avaṉ eṅkum ūṭuruvi niṟpavaṉāy virintiruttali ōruṅkāl, avaṉatu aruḷāṟṟal ivvulaka vuṭampu muḻumi yum nuḻintu, ataṟku oru kaḷikaṇāy niṉṟu ataṉi iyakkā niṟkiṉṟami iṉitu pulaṉākum. taṉṉāṟ paṭikkap paṭṭa paṭippiṉ ovvoru pirivilum avaṉ niṟintiruk kiṉṟaṉaṉ. avaṉ paṭittavaṟṟuḷ avaṉ irāta iṭamu milli, avaṉukku eṭṭāmal akaṉṟiruppatu milli, avaṉukkut teriyāmal nuṇṇitāyiruppatum illi, avaṉāl oru poruṭṭāka eṇṇap paṭātatum illi. aṟivuṭiyatu aṟivillatu eṉṟu pakukkappaṭṭa iruvaki yulakiṉ uṭporuḷilum avaṉatu aruḷ uḷ niṉṟu avaṟṟōṭu orumittirukkiṉṟatu. avaṉ ōriṭattiruntu maṟṟōr iṭattiṟkup peyarntu celvaṉāyiṉun, tāṉ paṭittavaṟṟuḷ ētēṉum oṉṟiliruntāvatu, aḷavupaṭātu parampivirinta ivveḷiyiṉkaṇ oru pakutiyiliruntāvatu taṉṉi vēṟu pirippaṉāyiṉum, aḵtu avaṉ varampil āṟṟaṟku ōr iḻukkākavē muṭiyum. curuṅkac colluṅkāl, iṟivaṉ eṅkumi rukkiṉṟāṉ eṉpatu maṭṭum viḷaṅku vataṉṟi, avaṉ etuvariyil niṟintu niṟkiṉṟāṉ eṉpatu oruvāṟṟā ṉum aṟiyappaṭuva tilliyeṉka.
iṉi, avaṉ eṅkumirukku miyalpuṭiyaṉātal pōla vē, ellām aṟiyum vallamiyu muṭiyaṉeṉpatum iṉitu viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. avaṉ eṅkum uṇmiyiṉālētāṉ ellām aṟiyum aṟivum iyaṟkiyāy iṉṟiyami yātatāyc curantu perukukiṉṟatu. aṅṅaṉam allākkāl avaṉ niṟintirukkum ivvulaka muḻumiyum naṭipeṟukiṉṟa toḻiṟ kūṟukaḷ ovvoṉṟaṉiyum avaṉ eṅṅaṉam aṟiyamāṭṭuvāṉ? tāṉ orumittirukkum uyirkaḷ ovvoṉṟi ṉiṭattum eḻukiṉṟa aṟiviṉ kūṟukaḷ ovvoṉṟaṉiyum eṅṅaṉam uṇaramāṭṭu vāṉ? cilar ivvulaka amippiṉip paṟṟip pēciyakkāl, iḵtu avaṟku ōr uṟiviṭamāmeṉṟum, avaṉatu aruḷ amiḻtam nirampuṅ koḷkalamāmeṉṟuṅ kūṟiṉārkaḷ. aṟivu nūlāciriyar vēṟoruvar, ivvirinta veḷḷiṭiyi uṟṟu ṇarntu iḵtu iṟivaṉatu aruḷuṇarvu amarntirukkum peruṅ kaḷamāmeṉṟu kūṟiṉārākaliṉ, aḵtoṉṟutāṉ mika vuyarnta mēlāṉa karuttāyt tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. tammōṭu aṇṭiyil niṟkum orucila poruḷkaḷiṉ iruppiyum iyakkattiyum aṟiyum aimpoṟi yuṇarviṉ iruppiṭam vilaṅkukaḷukkum makkaḷukkum uṇṭu. aṅṅaṉam avar aṟiyum aṟivum uṉṉippum mikak kuṟukiya ōriṭattiṉuḷ maṭṭumē cuḻaṉṟu nikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl iṟivaṉō tāṉ uṟikiṉṟa ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum aṟiyuṅ kaṭap pāṭu mēṟkoṇṭu viḷaṅkukiṉṟāṉākaliṉ, avaṉ eṅkumuḷḷa peruveḷiyē avaṉatu elliyaṟṟa aṟivu nikaḻccikku iṭantaruvatā meṉpatum, aḵtu avaṉatu muṟṟuṇarvu nikaḻccikku iṉṟi yamiyāta oru karuvipōl āvatām eṉpatum aṟital vēṇṭum.
uṭampōṭu oṟṟumippaṭṭut tōṉṟum ōr uyir ataṉiṉiṉṟum viṭupaṭṭu, maṉattiṉuṅ kaṭukic ceṉṟu ip paṭippiṉ elliyik kaṭantu, palakōṭi yāṇṭukaḷāka oru peṟṟippaṭa ēki ivvirinta veḷiyiṉ appāṟpōyk kaḻiyiṉum, atu piṉṉum iṟivaṉāl akattiṭappaṭṭut tiruvaruḷ veḷiyāl tāṉ cūḻappaṭṭiruttalit teṟṟeṉa aṟintu koḷḷum. yām uṭampoṭu kūṭi orumippaṭṭut tōṉṟukiṉṟa kālattum, avaṉ eṅ kaṭpulaṉukku viḷaṅkiṟ ṟilaṉāyiṉum, em akattē viḷaṅki yamarntiruk kiṉṟāṉ eṉpatu tiṇṇamēyām. itupaṟṟiyaṉṟē 
kārikkāl ammiyār,
 aṉṟun tiruvuruvaṅ kāṇātē yāṭpaṭṭēṉ
iṉṟun tiruvuruvaṅ kāṇkilēṉ - eṉṟuntāṉ
evvuruvō ṉumpirā ṉeṉpārkaṭ keṉṉurikkēṉ
evvuruvō niṉṉuruva mētu
eṉṟu aruḷic ceyvārāyiṉar. maṟṟōr aṉpar ō eṉ aracē, niṉṉi yāṉ eṅṅaṉaṅ kāṇpēṉ! niṉṉik kāṇum poruṭṭu muṉ ōṭiṉēṉ; aṅkum nī veḷippaṭukiṉ ṟili; piṉ ōṭiṉēṉ; aṅkum veḷippaṭukiṉṟili, nī paṇi ceyyum iṭappakkattiṟ ṟirumpiṉēṉ. aṅkuṅ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟili; valap pakkattiṟ ṟirumpiṉēṉ. yāṉ kāṇakkūṭā teṉṟē niṉṉi aṅkum oḷittuk koḷkiṉṟaṉi! niṉ kaḷḷam iruntavāṟeṉṉē! eṉṟu neñcaṅ kuḻintu viḷam piṉār. curuṅkac colluṅkāl, emakku, aṟiviṉuḷ aṟivāy niṉṟu avaṉ uṇarttu talāṉum, aruṭkuruvāyk kaṇmuṟ ṟōṉṟi aṟivuṟuttu talāṉum, avaṉi nāṅ kāṇāviṭiṉum, avaṉ emakkuḷ viḷaṅkutal orutaliyeṉṟu uṟutiyāka uṇarntēṉ.
iṅṅaṉam ellāmvalla iṟivaṉuṭiya aruḷ niṟivu aruḷaṟivukaḷiṉ eṅkumuḷḷa iyalpiṉi nāṉ āḻntu niṉikkiyil, āṟutal payavāmaṟṟi niṉivu kaḷellām eṉ uḷḷattiṉiṉṟum oruṅkē maṟintu oḻintaṉa. mutaṉmiyāy, eṅkē nammi iṟivaṉ kuṟiyātu viṭukiṉṟaṉaṉō eṉṟu añcukiṉṟa uyirkaḷiyum, iṉṉum maṟṟi ellā uyirt tokikaḷiyum avaṉ tiruvuḷam paṟṟātu iruppāṉ allaṉ. emakkuḷ nikaḻum ellā niṉivukaḷuḷḷum avaṉ maṟivāy eḻuntaruḷik, kuṟippāy eṉṉi ippōtu kavalippaṭuttu varuttukiṉṟa eṉṉeñ catti ṉuḷḷumiruntu aruḷamiḻtam poḻintu emmik kaṭikkaṇikkiṉṟāṉ. avaṉ taṉṉuṭiya uyirkaḷ evaṟṟiyum kaṭikkaṇiyātu viṭutal iyalāmiyāl, mikavun tāḻmiyāṉa uḷḷattuṭaṉ puḻuttalināyiṉuṅ kaṭiyē mākiya emmiyum oru poruṭṭāka em perumāṉ niṉivaṉō eṉṟu uruki urukik kuḻintaḻukiṉṟa nallaṉparkku, avaṉ ṟaṉatu aruṭkaṭikkaṇ nōkkañ cātti aruḷ purikiṉṟāṉ eṉpatil nām ciṟitum aiyuṟutal vēṇṭā.

3. murukavēḷ
kaṉaviṟkaṇṭa tuṉpamali
makkaṭku nērun tuṉpaṅkaḷ ellām oruṅku tiraṭṭi orutuṟip paṭuttu ellārkkum ēṟṟat tāḻviṉṟi oru nikarākap paṅkiṭappaṭumāyiṉ, tammi ippōtu nalviṉi yaṟṟavarāka niṉintiruppār palarum, aṅṅaṉan tamakkup paṅkiṭappaṭuṅ kūṟu peṟutaṟku muṉṉarē, tamakku muṉṉuḷḷatē pōtum pōtumeṉṟu kūṟiyamivar eṉṉum ōr ariya karuttic cāṉṟōr oruvar veḷiyiṭṭār. ēṉiyoru cāṉṟōr ik karuttiṉ āḻatti ārāyntu pārttu, emakku nērum iṭarkaḷun tuṉpaṅkaḷum maṟṟiyōṉ oruvaṉukku nērvaṉavaṟṟik kāṭṭiṉum, emmuṭiya nukarccikku icintaṉavāy irukkiṉṟaṉa veṉpatu avaṉuṭaṉ emakkuḷḷavaṟṟi māṟṟik koḷḷak kūṭumāyiṉ naṉku pulappaṭum eṉṟu iṉṉum nuṇukkamāka ataṉi eṭuttu moḻintiṭṭār.
ivviru karuttiṉiyum paṟṟi niṉintavāṟē nāṉ eṉatu cāymāṉak kuṟicciyil iruntapōtu, eṉṉi yaṟiyāmalē ayarntatōr uṟakkam eṉṉi vantu kavintatu. caṭutiyilē yāṉ eṉṟuṅkēḷāta oru puṉita oli eṉmukamāy vantatu. aḵteṉṉi eṉṟaṟiyak kaṇviḻittut teṉṟicip pakka māyk kāṇkiyiṟ, pacci mañcaḷip piḷantāṟpōlap pacuṅ katir poḻiyum aracirukki maṇṭapam oṉṟu tōṉṟiṟṟu; atu nāṟpakkaṅkaḷilum poṟcuvar eḻuppi mukaṭu poṟṟakaṭu vēyntu amikkappaṭṭiruntatu; koḻumiyāṉa cempavaḷat tāṟ kaṭintu tiraṭṭiya nūṟu tūṇkaḷ aṅku niṟuttapṭṭirun taṉa. kīḻē vaḻuvaḻuppāka iḻitta veḷḷic calavik kaṟka ḷiṉ poruttuvāyil nīlamaṇikaḷ aḻuttip patikkappaṭṭirun taṉa. mēlē, poṟcariki piṉṉiya veṇ paṭṭāṭi paṭaṅ kākak kaṭṭappaṭṭiruntatu. yāṉ innilavulakattiṟ kaṇṭaṟi yāta malarkaḷ āṅkāṅku viṭu pūvākac citaṟikkiṭantu oru puṉita maṇaṅ kamaḻntaṉa. ivvāṟu ellān teyvat taṉmiyuṭiyaṉa vāy viḷaṅkum am maṇṭapattiṉ naṭuviṟ kaṇṇoḷi maḻuṅka muḻumaṇi kuyiṟṟic ceyta ariyaṇiyoṉṟu iṭap paṭṭiruntatu. mutaṉ mutal ataṉip pārkkiyil ataṉmēl yārumiṉṟi atu vaṟitāyk kāṇappaṭṭatu. ivvāṟē am maṇṭapam eṅkum yārumiṉṟi veṟumi yāyiruntatu. iḵteṉ ṉi yeṉṟu viyantu uṟṟu nōkkukiyil, vikaṟi mūṭupaṉi yik kīṟikkoṇṭu iḷañāyiṟu taḷataḷaveṉṟu tōṉṟutal pōlap puṉita aruṭṭiru mēṉiyuṭiyavarāyuṅ, kātiṟ caṅkak kuḻi tulaṅkupavarāyum, muṟukkaviḻntu itaḻvirinta cen tāmari pōṉṟa mukamum aruḷ oḻuku viḻikaḷum viḷaṅkap poruppeṉa akaṉṟa tiruppavaḷa mārpil muttalivēl ēntiṉava rāyum, piṟikkoḻuntu nakuñ civanta caṭikkaṟṟi muṭiyuṭiyavarāyum uri varampamiyāp puriperun tōṟṟat tuṭaṉ aṟakkaṭavuḷ avvariyaṇi mītu amarn tirukkum nili kaṭpulaṉāyiṟṟu. uṭaṉē avar pakkattil nōkkiṉēṉ; āṇṭu uriyāl aḷappariya palavēṟu uruvaṅkaḷ uṭiya rāṉa paṇiyāḷarkaḷum piṟarum accak kuṟipput tōṉṟa āṅkāṅku vāyputittu varici variciyāy niṟṟaluṅ kaṇṭēṉ. avvaṟakkaṭavuḷukku etirē niṟikōl oṉṟu tūkkappaṭṭiruntatu. atukaṇṭu, naṭunili vaḻātu avaravar viṉi nukarccikaḷi aḷantaṟiyum akkaṭavuḷ tiruvuḷap pāṅkiṉi aṟivuṟuttum aṭiyāḷam ituvō eṉṟu niṉintēṉ. iṅṅaṉam yāṉ eṇṇik koṇṭirukkiṉṟa aḷavilē, ak kaṭavuḷ tan tiruvāy malarntu orupakkattu neruṅkiyiruntāri nōkki, ovvōr āṭavarun tattam iṭarkaḷiyun tuṉpaṅkaḷiyuṅ koṇṭupōy, utō! tōṉṟum iṭiveḷiyiṟ kuvikkak kaṭavar eṉṟu kaṭṭaḷi yiṭṭu, itaṟkeṉṟu varintu vikkappaṭṭirunta oru veḷi nilattik kuṟippiṭṭār. kuṟippiṭutalum, aṅku nikaḻvaṉa vaṟṟik kāṇṭaṟ poruṭṭu nāṉ virintu ceṉṟu ap poṭṭittiṭal naṭuvil niṉṟukoṇṭu mika makiḻntu nōkkumpoḻutu, aṅkuk kuḻumiyirunta makkaḷ aṉivarum oruvarpiṉ oruvarāy naṭantu vantu, tāntāñ cumantu pōnta palvakic cumikaḷiyum avveḷiyil iṟakkik kuvittaṉar. ak kuviyal muḻutum oṉṟu cērntu mukilmaṇṭilatti ūṭuruvik koṇṭu malipōl uyarvatāyiṟṟu.
aṅku akkaṭavuḷ iṭṭa kaṭṭaḷiyi naṭipeṟu vittaṟ poruṭṭup pūṅkompupōṉ melintu, ūtiṉāṟ paṟakkum uṭampiṉaḷāṉa ōr iḷampeṇ mikavuñ cuṟu cuṟuppāy aṅkumiṅku mulaviṉaḷ; avaḷ kiyoṉṟiṟ perukkak kaṇṇāṭi oṉṟiruntatu. avaḷ taḷara uṭuttirunta nīṇṭa āṭi kāṟṟil aliyumpoḻutu, ataṉ iṭiyē piṉṉappaṭṭirunta palvēṟu pēy urukkaḷuṅ kūḷi urukkaḷum āyiraṅkōḷāṟāṉa vaṭivaṅkaḷik kāṭṭiṉa. avaḷatu pārviyil ettakiyārum veruvutaṟkuriya koṭun taṭumāṟ ṟak kuṟippukaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. avaḷ peyar vīṇ eṇṇam eṉappaṭum. appeṇ aṅku ovvōr āḷukkum uriya cumiyiṉi nirampak kiḷarcciyōṭum potintu avartam tōḷkaḷil ēṟṟi oruvarpiṉ oruvarāy avarik kuṟippiṭṭa iṭattiṟkuc cellumpaṭi naṭattiṉāḷ. eṉṉōṭu oru piṟappiṉarāṉa makkaḷ iṅṅaṉan taṅkaṭaṅkaṭkuriya cumiyiṉic cumantavuṭaṉ avaṟṟiṉ poṟi poṟukka māṭṭāmaṟ kataṟu tali eṉ kaṇṇetirē kāṇavum, eṉakku muṉṉē kuvikkappaṭṭa makkaṭ ṭuṉpamaliyiṉip paṟṟi niṉikkavum eṉ neñcaṅ karintu urukiṟṟu.
iṅṅaṉamāyiṉum, annērattil aṅku vēṟu cilar ceyta ceykikaḷāl eṉakku mikunta kaḷippuṇṭāyiṟṟu. oruvaṉ oru paḻiya cariki yāṭiyil oru potiyi nirampa uṉṉippāy maṟitteṭuttu varutalik kaṇṭēṉ; vantavaṉ appotiyiṉi ak kuviyaṉmēl eṟikiyil atu vaṟumi eṉṟu terintēṉ. maṟṟoruvaṉ niṟinta cerukkoṭu naṭantu pōntu taṉ mūṭṭiyiṉi yeṟiya, ataṉi āyntu nōkkiṉēṟku aḵtavaṉ maṉik kiḻattiyāyk kāṇappaṭṭatu.
iṉṉum aṅkē kaṇiyun tīṅkoḻuntum niṟin taṉavum vīṇ eṇṇaṅ koṇṭaṉavum āṉa mūṭṭikaḷ cumantaṉarāyk kātaliḷiñar palar tokuti tokutiyāka varutaluṅ kaṇṭēṉ; iṅṅaṉaṅ kaṇṭa kāṭciyil uṇṭāṉa perum putumi eṉṉeṉṟāl avvāṟu vanta kātaluḷḷa muṭiyār apperiya iṭarppotiyiṉ poṟiyāl tam uḷḷam irupiḷavāy aḻivatupōl neṭṭuyirppeṟintaṉa rāyiṉum ak kuviyaliṉ arukē vantatun tam mūṭṭikaḷi eṟintuviṭa maṉamilarāy varunti niṉṟaṉar; piṉṉarc ciṟitu vīṇē varunti muyaṉṟu pārttapiṉ, tantalikaḷi acittuk koṇṭu vantatu pōlavē poṟi cumantaṉarāy appuṟam pōyiṉar. iṉṉum aṅkē perumutumakaḷir palar tammuṭam piṉ tirinta tōlkaḷi eṟintu viṭutalum, iḷiyamakaḷir palar tam muṭampiṉ paḻuppāṉa niṟamuṭiya tōli urittu eṟitaluṅ kaṇṭēṉ. iṉṉum aṅkē civanta mūkkukaḷum, parutta utaṭukaḷuṅ, kaṟippaṟkaḷum perum peruṅ kuviyalāyk kiṭantaṉa. ivvāṟu mika uyarntu ōṅkiya attuṉpa maliyiṟ perumpālum aḻakillāta uṭampi ṉuṟuppukaḷē viravik kiṭattalik kāṇṭalum, uṇmi yākavē eṉakkup periya tōr iṟumpūtu eḻuvatāyiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam nāṉ iṟum pūtuṟṟuk kāṇkiyil, oruvaṉ ēṉiyōrik kāṭṭilum perumpoṟi tāṅkiṉaṉāy av viṭarp poruppiṉ arukē neruṅkip peritu eḻunta makiḻcciyōṭu ataṉi vīci eṟintāṉ; eṟitalum, aḵtavaṉukku iyaṟkiyē mutukiluḷḷa pariya murippiṉ taciyeṉṟu kaṇṭukoṇṭēṉ. itupōlavē iṉṉum aṅku ellāvakiyāṉa uṭampiṉ cīrkēṭu kaḷum kuvikkap paṭṭiruntaṉa; eṉṟālum, accīrkēṭukaḷiṟ perumpālāṉa uṇmiyalla veṉpatūum, avaravartam vīṇ eṇṇattāṟ piṟantaṉavā meṉpatūum yāṉ iṉitaṟivēṉā yiṉēṉ. iṉi, makkaṭku iyaṟkiyē uṇṭākum nōykaḷ muḻumiyuñ cērnta kalavic ciṟupoṟi yovvoṉṟu aḻakiya makkaḷ palappalar kiyilum irukkak kaṇṭēṉ; ic ciṟupotiyiṉ peyar ciṉam eṉṟu collappaṭum. iṉi, iṅku ivi yellāvaṟṟiṉum eṉakkup periyatoru viyappiṉi yuṇṭupaṇṇiya toṉṟuṇṭu; aḵteṉṉi yeṉiṉ, im muḻukkuviyalilum maṭami oṉṟāvatu, pollāṅku oṉṟāvatu eṟiyappaṭavē illi: itaṉāl, aritiṟkiṭitta in nērattil makkaḷ ovvoruvarun taṅkaṭaṅkaṭku vēṇṭātaṉavā yuḷḷa kontaḷikkum viḻi vukaḷ maṉac cāyvukaḷ maṉanoymmikaḷ mutalāṉavaṟṟi vilakkik koḷḷukiṉṟilarē! eṉa uḷ niṉintu viyantēṉ.
ivaṟṟiṟku iṭiyē, tāṉ iḻitta kuṟṟaṅkaḷic cumantu koṇṭu vanta peruṅkāvāli oruvaṉik kuṟippāy nōkkiṉēṉ; avaṉ vīciya mūṭṭiyi ārāyntu pārkkiyil, avaṉ taṉ kuṟṟaṅkaḷi nīkki viṭuvataṟku maṉamiṉṟit taṉ niṉivi maṭṭum eṟintu pōyiṉāṉeṉpatu teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkiṟṟu. avaṟkup piṉṉē payaṉaṟṟa vīṇaṉāṉa paṭṭimakaṉ oruvaṉ virintu pōntu taṉ maṭamiyi eṟitalviṭṭut taṉ nāṇṭakimiyiyē vīcip pōyiṉāṉ.
iṅṅaṉam makkaṭtokuti muḻutuṅ tammuṭiya arum poṟikaḷi iṟakkic ceṉṟapiṉ, innērattil itaṉi naṭipeṟuvittaṟ poruṭṭu ulāvik koṇṭirunta oru cūr aṇaṅku taṉṉantaṉiyaṉāy ikkāṭciyiṉip pārttuk koṇṭu vaṟitēniṟkum eṉṉarukē vantatu; varutalum eṉakku oru maṉattaṭumāṟṟam uṇṭāyiṟṟu; caṭutiyil avaḷ taṉ kiyiṟ piṭittirunta perukkakkaṇṇāṭiyiṉi eṉ kaṇkaḷi ṉetirē nērākap piṭittaṉaḷ. atil uṭaṉē eṉ mukattik kāṇṭalum, mikac ciṟitāyirunta avvaṭivam ippōtu mikap peritāyt tōṉṟuvatik kaṇṭu maruṇṭēṉ. ivvetirt tōṟṟattiṉ varampu kaṭanta uṟuppiṉ akalatti nōkka nōkka eṉ uruvattiṉmēl eṉakkē veṟuppuṇṭāyiṟṟu; ataṉpiṉ ataṉi mukamūṭiyippōl eṉṉiṉiṉṟum vīciyeṟintēṉ. ippōtu eṉatu nalviṉiyāl eṉ arukē niṉṟa oruvaṉ taṉ mukam mika nīṇṭatāyiruntamikaṇṭu, ciṟitu muṉṉē ataṉi am makkaṭ ṭuṉpamalimēṟ piṭuṅki yeṟintu viṭṭu niṉṟukoṇ ṭiruntāṉ. uṇmiyilēyē avaṉ mukam nāṇat takkavakiyāy mika nīṇṭutāṉ iruntatu. vāymiyē pēcuṅ kāl, avaṉ mōvāy maṭṭum eṉ muḻu mukattiḷivu peritāyiruntatu eṉṟu nampukiṉṟēṉ. nāṅkaḷ iruvēmum eṅkaḷ uṟuppiṉ aḻakiṉmiyit tiruttik koḷvataṟku nallanēram vāyttatu. ellārum tāntān tara vēṇṭuvaṉa vaṟṟit tantu pōkaṭṭamiyāl, ovvoruvarum tamakkuḷḷa kuṟiyiṉi maṟṟip piṟaroṭu māṟṟikkoṇṭu niṟivu ceytukoḷḷa iṭam peṟuvārāyiṉar.
iṅṅaṉam makkaḷellārum tattamakkuriya tuṉpaṅ kaḷiṉiṉṟum viṭutipeṟṟami kaṇṭu uriyaḷavu kaṭanta uvaki eytiṉēṉ. ivvāṟu appoḻutu nāṅkaḷ ellēmum akkuviyalic cūḻntu niṉṟu koṇṭu, atiṟcērntu kiṭantavaṟṟip pārttēmāyiṉum, tamakku vāṇāḷiṟ peṟum iṉpamum nalamum ik kuviyalil ivitā meṉṟu terintu koḷḷāmal niṉṟōr ap peruṅkuḻāttil yārumē ilar. ippaṭiyāyiṉ, ivaṟṟiṟku iyaṟkiyilē urimiyuṭiyōr ivaṟṟit tuṉpuṟuttum perum poṟiyākak koṇṭavā ṟeṉṉiyeṉṟu niṉintu niṉintu viyappaṭintēṉ.
ivvāṟu, ittuṉpak kuḻampaliyum alicaliyuṅ karuttāyp pārttu eṇṇiyavaṇṇamāy yāṉ niṟkiyil, attāṇi maṇṭapat tariyaṇimītu amarntaruḷiya aṟak kaṭavuḷ tan tiruvuḷamuvantu, ippōtu ovvoruvarun tān tām virumpumāṟu tamakkuḷḷa tuṉpatti māṟṟi, akkuviyali ṉiṉṟum piritteṭuttuk koṭukkap paṭuvati mūṭṭik kaṭṭik koṇṭu tamatu iruppiṭañ cēralām eṉṟu iraṇṭāmuṟiyum oru kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭaruḷiṉār.
ikkaṭṭaḷi piṟattalum, vīṇ eṇṇam eṉṟa appeṇ tirumpavuñ cuṟucuṟuppākat toḻiṉ muyaṟciyiṟ pukuntu, attuṉpakkuviyal muḻumiyum avaravarkku vēṇṭum vakiyāṟ pakuttuppakuttuc cumi kaṭṭi, nampaviyalāta virivuṭaṉ avaravarkkuriya mūṭṭiyi avaravar tōḷmēl ēṟṟi viṭṭāḷ. ippōtu kiḷampiya alicaliyum viricaliyum aḷa viṭṭurittal muṭiyātu. innērattil nāṉ kaṇṭaṟinta cilavaṟṟi ulakiṟku veḷiyiṭukiṉṟēṉ. pōṟṟak takka narittōṟṟam uṭiyaṉāṉa mutiyōṉ oruvaṉ taṉakkirunta aṭivayiṟṟu nōyi vīci eṟinta piṉ, taṉ kāṇi yāṭcikku urimi peṟutaṟ poruṭṭu eṭuppuppiḷḷi vēṇṭiyiruntamiyiṉ, akkuviyalil tantiyāl vekuṇṭu eṟiyappaṭṭa kaṭaṉ ṟavaṟiya makaṉ oruvaṉi virinteṭuttaṉaṉ. aṅṅaṉan terinteṭukkappaṭṭa naṉṟi yillā iḷiñaṉ kālmaṇi nērattiṟkuḷ ammutiyōṉ tāṭiyip piṭittu iḻuttu avaṉ mūḷiyit tiṟantu citaṟi aṭikka virumpiṉāṉ. itaṉāl veruk koṇṭa ammutiyōṉ, vayiṟṟip picinta vaṇṇamāy varuntit taṉṉetirē vanta appiyaṉ tanti yip pārttu niṉ makaṉi eṭuttukkoṇṭu peyarttum eṉ vayiṟṟuvaliyi eṉakkuk koṭuttu viṭu eṉṟu mika irantu vēṇṭiṉaṉ. vēṇṭiyum eṉṉi? avar iruvarun tām terinteṭuttuk koṇṭavaṟṟi maṟittun tama teṇṇap paṭiyē māṟṟik koḷvataṟku valivaṟṟavarā ṉārkaḷ. tōṇiyil taṇṭuvalikkum vēliyilirunta ēḻi aṭimi oruvaṉ taṉ kāl vilaṅkiṉik kaḻaṟṟi yeṟintu viṭṭu, ataṟku māṟākap poruttup piṭippu nōyit terintu eṭuttuk koṇṭaṉaṉ. koṇṭapiṉ mukañcuḷittu mika varuntiṉa miyāl, avaṉum ikkoṭukkal vāṅkalil ūtiyam peṟṟā ṉallaṉeṉpatu nōkkuvārkkellām eḷitu viḷaṅkum. iṉṉum ivvāṟē, aṅku vaṟumikku māṟāka nōyiṉiyuñ ceriyāmikku māṟākap paciyiṉiyum, tuṉpattiṟku māṟākak kavaliyiṉiyuṅ kikkoṇṭu palavakip paṇṭamāṟṟukaḷ nikaḻntami kaṇṭu mikavun kaḷippuṇṭāyiṟṟu.
iṉip, peṇtokuppārun tammuḷ oruvaroruvark kuriya uṟuppiṉvaṭivukaḷi māṟṟik koṇṭu nirampavum muyaṟciyāy vāṇikañ ceytaṉar. orutti taṉ orupiṭi narimayirik koṭuttu oru piḷavi kaṭṭiyi vāṅkik koṇṭāḷ; maṟṟorutti taṉ ciṟṟiṭiyiṉik koṭuttu tiraṇṭuruṇṭa iraṇṭu tōḷkaḷi eṭuttukkoṇṭāḷ. vēṟorutti taṉ kaṟpuṭimiyiṉit tantu taṉ aḻakukeṭṭa mukattiṉi tirutti vāṅkikkoṇṭāḷ; āyiṉum, iṅṅaṉañ ceytuḻiyellām putuk kuṟippāṭṭiṉukku urimi koṇ ṭār palaruḷḷum oruvarēṉum aṭutta nērattilēyē atu paḻiya kuṟiyik kāṭṭiṉum iṭarp payappatātali viḷaṅka aṟiyāmi illi. ivvāṟē akkuḻuviluḷḷār ov voru varun tamakkirunta paḻaṅkuṟikku māṟākap peṟṟa putuk kuṟiyiṉāl makiḻtaliṉṟi oru taliyāka iṭaruḻat taliyē kaṇṭu varuntiṉēṉ. āṉālum, namakku eytum iṭarkaḷ aṉiyavum nām poṟukkumaḷavil nam valivukku iciya aḷavāka vakuttup poruttap paṭukiṉṟaṉavō, aṉṟi avi paḻakap paḻaka nammāl tāṅkappaṭum iyaṟkiyavāy iṇaṅku kiṉṟaṉavō iṉṉateṉṟu niliyiṭutaṟkup pukuntēṉ.
iṉi, aṅku muṉmoḻinta kūṉaṉ oruvaṉ taṉ mutukilirunta imilip pōkkiviṭṭuc ciṟunīrp piyiṟ kalloṉṟu iṭuvittukkoṇṭu uruvam mikattirunti oḻuṅkāyp pōtaliyum, ivaṉōṭu ivvāṟu māṟṟik koṇṭa aḻakiṟciṟantāṉ oruvaṉ tāṉ muṉṉē makaḷirāl naṉku pukaḻappaṭṭa aḻakiya nalluruvatti iḻantu, ammakaḷir kūṭṭattiṉūṭē taṉ talikkumēl taṉ iraṇṭu tōḷkaḷum pituṅkak kūṉi naṭantu pōtaliyuṅ kaṇṭu irakkam mikuntu eṉ neñcam nekkuṭintatu.
itaṟkiṭiyil nāṉ eytiya ūtiyattiṉ iyal piṉiyuṅ kūṟātuviṭutaliṉṟi viḷampi viṭukiṉṟēṉ. nīṇṭa mukamuṭiya ṉāṉa eṉ naṇpaṉ eṉ ciṟiya mukattit tāṉ vāṅkik koṇṭa tuṇiyāṉē mikavuṅ kōṇaṅkiyāṉa vaṭivamu ṭiyaṉāy niṟṟalik kāṇṭalum, eṉ mukatti nāṉē koṭuttu avaṉi veṭkappaṭutti viṭṭataṉi eṇṇit tāṅkamuṭiyāmaṟ cirippēṉāyiṉēṉ; aṉṉō! an naṇpaṉ nāṉ pakaṭipaṇṇutalit terintu koṇṭu, tāṉ ceytatip paṟṟi mikavum nāṇa muṟuvā ṉāyiṉaṉ; maṟṟu, eṉ tiṟatti ārāykiyil nāṉpeṟṟa veṟṟiyik kuṟittu nāṉ uvappataṟkum iṭamillāmaṟ pōyiṟṟu; eṉṉi? eṉ neṟṟiyit toṭutaṟku virumpi virali uyarttukiyil avviral avviṭattit tappi eṉ mēlitaḻiṉ mēṟ ṟeṟṟuppaṭṭatu. aḵtallāmalum, eṉ mūkku mika nīṇṭuviṭṭa miyil eṉ mukattit taṭavip pārttu ataṉ vēṟu oru pakutiyit toṭukiyi ḷellām iraṇṭu mūṉṟumuṟi eṉ ki mūkkiṉ mēṟ ṟaṭṭuppaṭṭu nōyi uṇṭākkiṟṟu. ivvāṟē eṉṉarukil niṉṟa vēṟiru celvarkaḷum eṉṉip pōlavē ēḷaṉamāṉa niliyi yeytiṉarāy niṟṟaluṅ kaṇṭēṉ. ivar iruvarun taṅkaṭkuḷḷa taṭittu vaḷinta iraṇṭu kālkaḷiyuṅ keṇṭic catiyiṉṟi melintu tōlkaḷ pōl nīṇṭa maṟṟiraṇṭu kālkaḷiyun tamatu maṭamit taṉmiyāl māṟṟikkoṇṭu niṉṟaṉar. ivaruḷ oruvaṉ taṉṉiyaṟki yuyarampōy ūṉṟukōlip paṟṟi ataṉ mēl naṭappavaṉ pōl mika uyarntu nīṇṭami yāl, avaṉ tali vāṉattilē kāṟṟiṟ cuḻalvatupōl tōṉṟiṟṟu; maṟṟoruvaṉ tāṉ naṭakka muntukiyil taṉ putiya kālkaḷ vaḷintu vaḷintu cuḻaṉṟamiyāl evvāṟu naṭappateṉṟē teriyāmal varuntiṉāṉ. ivaṉip pārttalum pakaṭi paṇṇa vēṇṭumeṉṉum viruppam eṉakku eḻuntamiyāl, eṉ kiyilirunta pirappaṅ kōli nilattil ūṉṟi nērāka oru kōṭu iḻuttu ikkōṭṭiṉ vaḻiyē nērāka naṭantu ik kōlaṇṭi kālmaṇi nērattil varukuviyāyiṉ itu taruvēṉ eṉṟu, tīviya koṭi muntirippaḻam piḻinta cāṟaṭitta kuppi oṉṟi vittuk pakaṭi paṇṇi makiḻntēṉ.
kaṭippaṭiyāka avviṭarkkuviyal muḻutum āṇpeṇ eṉṉum irupālarkkum pakuttiṭap paṭṭa piṉṉart, tattamakkuriya palvakip poṟiyiṉiyun tāṅkamāṭṭāmaṟ cumantu koṇṭu aṅkumiṅkuñ celvārāṉa am makkaḷi nōkkukiyil avar tiṟam peritum iraṅkattakkatāyiruntatu. avveḷi nilameṅkum muṇumuṇuppum, muṟiyīṭum, pulampalum aḻukiyum nirampiṉa. itaṉik kaṇṭa aṟak kaṭavuḷ avvēḻikaḷ mītu peritun tiruvuḷam iraṅki, avar tamakku iyaṟkiyilē yuriyavaṟṟi maṟupaṭiyum īntaruḷutaṟ poruṭṭu iraṇṭāmuṟiyum avartam poṟikaḷi yiṟakki nilattē uykkumpaṭi kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭa ruḷiṉār. aruḷalum, avarellāṅ katumeṉap pēruvaki eyti aṅṅaṉamē ceytaṉar; ataṉpiṟaku, muṉṉē avarkaḷ ellārkkum periyatoru māyaṅ kāṭṭi, avarkaḷi ataṉkaṇ viṭuttu ēmāṟṟiya cūrmakaḷ aṅkiruntavāṟē maṟintoḻiyumpaṭi kaṭṭaḷiyiṭap peṟṟāḷ. avaṭku māṟāka muṟṟum vēṟāṉa vaṭivamuṭiya nalla oru teyvamakaḷ varuvāḷāyiṉaḷ; avaḷatu oḻukalāṟu nērmiyum amitiyumuṭiyatāyirun tatu. avaḷatu vaṭivam poṟiyum uḷḷak kaḷippumuṭiyatāy viḷaṅkiṟṟu. avaḷ ciṟitu nērattiṟku orumuṟi māṉ maruṭṭun taṉ viḻikaḷāl vāṉmēl nōkki, avvaṟak kaṭavuḷip pullippārttaṉaḷ. avaḷ peyar poṟumi eṉappaṭum. at teyvamakaḷ at tuṉpamaliyiṉ arukē aṇuki niṉṟa vuṭaṉē, aṅku eṉakku mikavum viyappākak kāṇappaṭṭatu eṉṉiyō veṉiṉ, mikap peruttuyarnta ak kuviyaṉ muḻutuṅ kuṟukic curuṅki muṉṉirunta parumaṉil mūṉṟiloru kūṟukūṭak kāṇap paṭuvatiṉṟāyiṟṟu. ataṉpiṉ avaḷ ovvoruvarkkun takkavāṟu, uriya tuṉpaṅkaḷit tirumpak koṭuttu, avaṟṟi eḷitilē ēṟṟapaṭi nukarntoḻikkum naṉmuṟiyiṉiyum avarkkuk kaṟpittu viṭuttaṉaḷ. viṭuppavē, avarkaḷ tāmē muṉterin teṭuttataṉāl tamakku vantu nērnta iṭaril akappaṭātu, aṟiviṟ cāṉṟa am makaḷiṉ colluṟuti vaḻippaṭṭu oḻuka icintatu paṟṟi mikavuvantu ceṉṟaṉar.
it tōṟṟattiṉiṉṟum naṉku teḷiyaṟ pālaṉavām naṉṉeṟittiṟaṅkaḷ pala uḷavāyiṉuñ, ciṟappāka itaṉāl, nāṉ aṟintaṉa; eṉakku nērun tuṉpaṅkaḷi nukartaṟku añci yāṉ maṉakkuṟi koḷḷal eṉṟum ākāteṉpatūum, piṟarkku uḷavām iṉpaṅkaḷik kaṇṭu maṉamaḻuṅkal poruntā teṉpatūumēyām; eṉṉi? oruvaṉ, ayalā ṉoruvaṉ eytun tuṉpaṅkaḷiṉ iyaṟkiyi uḷḷavāṟu aḷantaṟital ēlāmiyāṉeṉka. inta ētuviṉik kaṭippiṭittē, piṟar iṭum muṟiyīṭukaḷi mika eḷiyaṉavāka niṉippatu kūṭāteṉṟum, eṉṉurimi yuṭaṉpiṟappiṉarāṉa makkaḷ eytun tuyaraṅkaḷippaṟṟi irakkamum maṉavurukkamum uṟak kaṭavēṉ eṉṟum uṟuti ceytu koṇṭēṉ.

4. marāṭaṉ kaṇṭa kāṭci
āṇ peṇ eṉṉum irupālārkku maṭṭumēyaṉṟi, vilaṅkukaḷukkum puṟpūṇṭukaḷukkum, uyirillātaṉa vākiya marakkaṭṭi kaṟkaḷukkum, iṉṉum ellāt tōṟṟaṅ kaḷukkumē uyir uṇṭeṉṟu amerikka tēyattu makkaḷ nampivarukiṉṟaṉar; kittoḻilāṟ ceyyappaṭuṅ katti, paṭaku, kaṇṇāṭi mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkum aḵtu uṇṭeṉṟu nampukiṉ ṟārkaḷ. ip poruḷkaḷil ētēṉum oṉṟu aḻiyuṅ kālattu, ataṉ āviyāṉatu, āṇpeṇkaḷiṉ āvikaḷ irukkum vēṟōr ulakattiṟkuc cellukiṉṟateṉavum niṉikkiṉṟaṉar. inta ētuppaṟṟiyē taṅkaḷ naṇparkaḷ iṟantāl, avarkaḷ tām uyirōṭirunta kālattu marattāṟ camitta villuṅ kaṇiyu mutalāṉa karuvikaḷi immiyulakil vaḻaṅkiyatu pōlavē, avarkaḷ maṟumiyulakilum avaṟṟiṉ āvikaḷik kiyāḷum poruṭṭu, avaṟṟi eppōtum avarkaḷatu cavattiṉ pakkattē vippar. ittakiya koḷki ettuṇip poruntātāyt tōṉṟiṉum, nantēyattuc cāṉṟōrkaḷum ivi pōl muḻutum nampa viyalāta pala koḷkikaḷiyum nilināṭṭiyirukkiṉṟaṉar: kuṟippāyc caṅkaramata vaḻip paṭṭavarkaḷ maṉa vulakattip paṟṟip pēcuṅkāl veṟiya eṇṇattiṉ aḷavāyuḷḷa poruḷkaḷiyum uyirkaḷiyum paṟṟiyē uriyāṭak kāṇkiṉṟā maṉṟō? pāṟkariya matavāti kaḷuṭ pallōrum iṅṅaṉamē tāṅ kūṟum poruḷkaḷi viḷaṅkā vakiyāy virittup pēcukiṉṟaṉar. itaṟku ōr eṭuttukkāṭṭāka ōr aṟiñar kāntak kallippaṟṟit tām viritteḻutiya ōr uriyil ak kalliṉ iḻukkum āṟṟa lāṉatu tīyiṉ cērkkiyāl aḻintu pōm eṉpataṉi nili niṟuttuvāṉ pukuntu, avar tām orunāḷ koḻuntu viṭ ṭerinta nilakkarit tokutiyiṉiṭiyil oru kāntakkalli iṭuvittu uṉṉippāy nōkkukiyil atiliruntu nīlavaṇṇa māṉa oru puki tiraṇṭu mēl eḻumpuvatip pārttu, appukiyē akkalliṉ uṇmi vaṭiva mākumeṉa nampi uri kūṟiṉār; avar avvāṟu kūṟiya akkalliṉ pukivaṭivē ataṉ uyirākum eṉpatūum avaratu karuttāyiṟṟeṉka.
iṉi, āvivaṭivaṅkaḷ tokuti tokutiyāyirukkum ōriṭattiṟku - atāvatu maṟumi yulaka meṉṟu iṅkuḷḷa nām vaḻaṅkukiṟa avviṭattiṟkut taṅkaḷ tēyattāṉ oruvaṉ kaṉāviṟpōy vantaṉaṉāka amerikkar tamakkuḷ oru kati vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. avaṉ aṅṅaṉam pōy mīṇṭa piṉṉar, iṟantōr uṟiyum avviṭattil tāṉ kaṇṭaṉa muṟṟun teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkat taṉ naṇparkaṭku eṭuttukkūṟiṉaṉ. itaṉip paṟṟip paḻami toṭṭu  avarkaḷ tamakkuḷ evvakiyāṉa varalāṟu vaḻaṅki varukiṟārka ḷeṉpatik, kūṭumāyiṉ, ucāvit terintukoḷḷumpaṭi avvamerikka tēyattu maṉṉarkaḷpāl moḻipeyarp pāḷaṉāyirukkum oruvaṉi eṉ naṇpaṉ oruvaṉ ēviṉāṉ. avaṉ avarkaḷip palamuṟiyum ucāviya palavaki viṉākkaḷāl terintu koṇṭa aḷavu atu piṉvarumāṟu eḻutap paṭalāyiṟṟu:-
akkāṭciyik kaṇṭu mīṇṭa marāṭaṉ eṉpāṉ oruvaṉ kuṭivāka irukkum oru maliyaṭivārattil neṭuvaḻi naṭantu ceṉṟapiṉ āvivaṭivaṅkaḷ taṅkum ulakattiṉ elliyiṟ kaṭiciyāka vantu cērntāṉ; av velliyiṟ kuṟuñ ceṭikaḷum, muṭceṭikaḷuṅ kūriya muṭkaḷum oṉṟō ṭoṉṟu caṉṉal piṉṉalākap piṇintatōr aṭarnta kāṭu iruntamiyāl ataṉiṭiyē vaḻikaṇṭu nuḻivatu kūṭātā yiṟṟu. avaṉ akkāṭṭiṉ eppakkattilāvatu cuvaṭupaṭṭa oṟṟaṭip paṭṭam ētēṉum uṇṭāveṉa uṟṟup pārttuk koṇṭirukkiyil, ataṉ oru pakkattil taṉakkuriya iri yit tēṭi ataṉip patuṅkip pārkkumāṟu pōlavē taṉmēl viḻivittup paṭuttuk koṇṭirukkum niliyil oruperuṅ kōḷariyik kaṇṭāṉ; kāṇṭalun, tiṭukkiṭṭup piṉṟaṅkiṉāṉ; avvarimāvum uṭaṉē tuḷḷi eḻuntu avaṉ mēṟ pāyntatu. appōḻtu taṉṉiṭattu ētoru paṭik kalamum illāmiyāl, kīḻkkiṭanta oru peruṅkallit taṉkiyileṭuppataṟkukkuṉintāṉ; kuṉintu paṟṟukiyil aḵtavaṉ kiyiṟ paṟṟappaṭātu pōkavē avaṉ peritum viyappuṟṟu, aḵtu oru kalliṉ veṟuntōṟṟamē eṉṟu kaṇṭāṉ. iṅṅaṉam itil ivaṉ ēmāntu pōṉālum, maṟṟa vakiyil mika makiḻvatāṉāṉ; avaṉṟaṉ iṭatu tōḷi vantu paṟṟiya av varimā ataṉip puṇpaṭutta māṭṭā tāyiṟṟu; aḵtu akkolivilaṅkiṉ pēyurut tōṟṟa meṉṟē avaṉ kaṇṭu koṇṭāṉ. vali viḻanta appaki vilaṅkiṉiṉṟun tappi avaṉ akkāṭṭaṇṭi pōy ataṉic ciṟitu nēram uṟṟup pārttapiṉ, maṟṟip pakkattik kāṭṭilum alicalāyirunta oru pākattiṉ vaḻiyāyt taḷḷi uḷ nuḻivataṟku muṉṉiṉāṉ. muṉṉit taḷḷalum ap putalkaḷ avaṉkiyi lakappaṭātu vaṟiyavāyirunta miyāṟ, piṉṉum mika viyappaṭintu, tiṟantaveḷiyil naṭappatupōl attuṇi eḷitāka muṭkaḷiṭiyilum muṭceṭikaḷiṭi yilum naṭantuceṉṟāṉ. curuṅkac colluṅ kāl, akkāṭu muḻuvatūuṅ kāṭṭiṉ veṟun tōṟṟamē yaṉṟip piṟitilli eṉka. ākavē, muṭceṭikaḷum muḷḷum pampiya intap peruṅkuṟuṅkāṭu akattē taṅkum āvikaḷukku ōr araṇāyk kōliya pacci maravēliyē yākumeṉṟum, immuṭceṭi kaḷum muḷḷiṉ kūriya muṉikaḷum ūṉuṅ kurutiyum nirampiya vuṭampik kiḻikkum valiyilavāyiṉum, mika nuṇṇiya āviyiṉ uṭampukaḷik kīṟa vallaṉavēyā meṉṟum avaṉ uṭaṉē muṭivu ceytāṉ. ivvakiyāṉa niṉivōṭum intac cikkalāṉa kāṭṭiṉ ūṭē pukuntu pōvataṟkut tīrmāṉañ ceytu, ivaṉ uḷḷē cellac cellat tīviya maṇaṅkamaḻun teṉṟaṟkāṟṟum varavara mikuti yāyum iṉitāyun taṉmēl vantu vīcutali uṇarntāṉ. ivvāṟu ciṟitu vaḻi naṭantu pōvataṟkuḷ muṭkaḷum muṭceṭikaḷum vaḷarnta iṭam muṭintupōka, naṟumaṇaṅ kamaḻvaṉavum niṟañciṟantaṉavumākiya malarkaḷāl mūṭappaṭṭa āyirakkaṇakkāṉa paciyamaraṅkaḷ aḻakuvāyntu niṟṟaliṉāl iṉiyaṉa palavum nirampiya kāṭāyttōṉṟum piṟitu ōriṭan, tāṉ muṉ kaṭantu pōnta muḷḷaṅkāṭṭiṟkuk karivakuttāṟpōl irukkak kaṇṭāṉ. iṉimi vāynta akkāṭṭiṭatti viṭṭup puṟappaṭṭu, ataṉāṟ cūḻappaṭṭa tiṟanta veḷiyiṟ pukuntapōtu, taṉ arukē kutiri vayavar palar kaṭukic cellakkaṇṭu, ataṉpiṟ ciṟitunēraṅ kaḻittu nāyk kūṭṭaṅ kulikkum ōci kēṭṭāṉ. itukēṭṭu muṭivataṟkuṭ, pāl pōlum veḷiyatoru kutiriyuruviṉ mutukumēl ōr iḷiñaṉ amarntukoṇṭu, urikkalākāta virivuṭaṉ, tamakku muṉ ōṭāniṉṟa oru muyaliṉ uruviṉi vēṭṭaṅkoḷḷat toṭarum nūṟu vēṭṭi nāykaḷiṉ āvikaḷip piṉpaṟṟip pāyntu cellak kaṇṭāṉ. anta veḷḷik kutirimēṟ pōnta iḷiñaṉ taṉ maruṅkē aṇukiyapōtu avaṉi naṉṟāy uṟṟuppārttu, avaṉṟaṉ arum perun taṉmikaḷi niṉintu niṉintu amerikka tēyattu mēṟpakutikaḷil uḷḷār ellārum appōtu eṅkum pulamputaṟ kētuvāy ariyāṇṭiṟkumuṉ iṟantupōṉa niccarākavaṉ eṉṉum araciḷam putalvaṉā yirukkak kaṇṭāṉ.
iṉi, avaṉ akkāṭṭi viṭṭu akaṉṟa piṉṉarp, pūkkaḷ miṭinta veḷi nilaṅkaḷiyum, pacum puṉṉilaṅ kaḷiyum, ōṭukālkaḷiyuṅ, katiravaṉ veḷiccam paṭuṅ kuṉṟukaḷiyum, niḻalpaṭarnta paḷḷat tākkukaḷiyuṅ kaṇṭu, tāṉaṟinta coṟkaḷā lāvatu piṟarkkuḷḷa karuttukkaḷā lāvatu avaṟṟiṉ ciṟappi viritturikka lākāteṉa eṇṇi uvap peytiṉāṉ. ivvaḷavu iṉpamāṉa iṭattil aḷavi ṟanta āvi urukkaḷ tattam eṇṇañ ceṉṟavāṟu tokuti tokutiyāka viḷiyāṭṭayartaluñ cilampam paḻakutaluñ ceytu uṟintu varalāyiṉa. av vāvikaḷiṟ cila ōr iruppuccilli aṅku miṅkumāka eṟintu koṇṭiruntaṉa; vēṟu cila oru kuṟuntaṟiyi ṉuruvatti ūṉṟik koṇṭirun taṉa; maṟṟuñ cila oru kutiriyi ṉuruvip paḻakkik koṇṭiruntaṉa; iṉṉum pala iṟantupōṉa karuvikaḷiṉ (amerikka tēyattu makkaḷ tām puḻaṅkiya karuvikaḷ uṭintāyiṉum erintāyiṉum pōṉāl avi iṟantaṉa veṉṟē taṅkaḷ moḻiyiṟ collu kiṟārkaḷ) urukkaḷik koṇṭu tiṟami vāynta kit toḻilkaḷ purivatiṟ kūṭṭaṅ kūṭṭamāyt taliyiṭṭiruntaṉa. peru makiḻcci payappatāṉa ikkāṭciyik kaṇṭu celkiyil, mikap pala vakiyāṉa niṟa vēṟupāṭ ṭōṭuñ ceḻippuṟṟut taṉṉic cuṟṟi eṅkum vaḷarntu eḻuntuniṟkum naṟumalarkaḷil perumpālaṉa, taṉ nāṭṭilun tāṉ eññāṉṟum pārātaṉavāyiruntamiyāl, avaṟṟip paṟippataṟkut tāṉ palamuṟiyum avāviṉāṉ. āṉālō, avi kāṭcip pulaṉām poruḷkaḷē yallāmal toṭṭaṟi taṟku iyalā veṟun tōṟṟamēyeṉac ciṟitu nērattuḷ terintu koṇṭāṉ. kaṭiciyāka avaṉ oru periya yāṟṟaṅ karip pakkattil vantu, tāṉē mīṉpiṭittaṟ ṟoḻilil vallavaṉātalāl, aṅkē tūṇṭiṟkāra ṉoruvaṉ palatiṟappaṭṭa pala aḷavāṉa mīṉkaḷip piṭittu iṭa, avi avaṉarukē mēluṅ kīḻumāyt tuḷḷik koṇṭiruttalip pārkkum poruṭṭuk karimēṟ ciṟitunēram niṉṟāṉ.
marāṭaṉ eṉṉum inta amerikkaṉ taṉ ṟēyattiluḷḷa makaḷiriṟ pēraḻakāṟ ciṟanta oru maṅkiyi muṉṉē maṇantā ṉeṉpatum, aḷavāṟ pala piḷḷikaḷip peṟṟāṉeṉpatum yāṉ muṉṉamē kuṟittirukkavēṇṭum. iv viruvarum māṟāk kātaṟ kiḻamiyōṭum, oruvar oruvarpāṟ ṟiṇṇitākap piṇikkappaṭṭa uḷḷattōṭum pukaḻpeṟa vāḻntami paṟṟi, avaṉ tēyattār vatuvi yayarum maṇamakaṉ maṇamakaḷi vāḻttuṅkāl, marāṭaṉum aṟāttilli yumpōl orumittu vāḻakkaṭavīrkaḷ eṉṟu iṉṟukāṟum vāḻt turi pakarvatu vaḻakkam. marāṭaṉ mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu accempa ṭavaṉ pakkattiṟ ciṟitu nērantāṉ niṉṟiruppaṉ; avvaḷavil, taṉ māṟākkātali aṟāttilliyi ṉuruvikkaṇṭu, avaḷi avaṉ iṉṉa ḷeṉṟu terintu koḷḷutaṟku muṉ, avaṉ mēl avaḷ taṉ kaṇṇic ciṟitunēram patiya vittup pārttaṉaḷ; uṭaṉē avaḷ kikaḷ avaṉit taḻuvutaṟku nīṇṭaṉa. avaḷ kaṇkaḷil nīr āṟāyp perukivaḻintatu, avaḷ pārviyum avaḷ kikaḷum avaḷ kuralum avaṉit taṉmāṭṭu aḻittaṉa; avviyāṟu kaṭakkalākā arumit teṉṟu atē nērattil terippaṉavum pōṉṟaṉa. taṉ aṉpiṟkuriya aṟāttilliyik kaṇṭa vaḷavāṉē av vamerikka ṉuḷḷatti leḻunta kaḷippu, tuyar, kātal, viruppu, viyappu eṉṟa uṇarviṉ ṟokutiyi evartām viḷakki yurikkavallār? avaḷip pārtta poḻutu avaṉ taṉ kaṉṉaṅkaḷil yāṟupōl oḻuki ōṭiya kaṇṇīrā laṉṟip piṟitoru vāṟṟāṉum ataṉit terinturikkamāṭṭā ṉāyiṉāṉ; inta niliyil avaṉ neṭunēram niṉṟilaṉ; uṭaṉē taṉ etirē yirunta nīrōṭ ṭattiṟ kutittu vīḻntāṉ; vīḻtalum, aḵtoru yāṟṟiṉ tōṟṟamā yiruntataṉṟi vēṟal lāmi kaṇṭu ataṉaṭiyil naṭantu pōy maṟṟip pakkattuk karimēl ēṟiṉāṉ. aṅṅaṉam avaṉ aṇukutalum aṟāttilli avaṉip paṟantuvantu taḻuviṉāḷ; taḻuvavē, tāṉum avaḷit taḻuvavirumpiyum, atu kūṭāvāṟu taṭi ceyta taṉatu paruvuṭampiṉ poṟiyiṉiṉṟum viṭupaṭutaṟku avāviṉāṉ. oruvari oruvar palakāl ucāviyum aṉpu kāṭṭiyum aḷavaḷāviya piṉṉar, avaḷ malar malinta avviṭattiṟ peṟap paṭuvaṉavākum ellā oppaṉikaḷum poruntumāṟu taṉ kiyāṟ camitta oru koṭippantarukku avaṉi aḻittuc ceṉṟāḷ. avaḷ ataṉi maṉak kaṟpaṉikkum eṭṭāvaṇṇam makiḻvuṟac ceytu nāṭōṟum putituputitāka ētēṉum oṉṟi ataṉoṭu cērttuk koṇṭē vantāḷ. marāṭaṉ avaḷatu anta uṟiyuḷiṉ urikkalākā aḻakiṉi viyantu, ataṉ eppakkattumiruntu vīcum naṟumaṇatti nukarntu kaḷippāl meymmaṟantu niṟ kiyil, aṟāttilli avaṉi nōkki: nīr iṟivaṉiṭattu vittirukkum meyyaṉpum, makkaḷ ellāriṭattum nīr uṇmiyāy oḻukumiyalpum numatu vāḻnāḷ eppoḻutu muṭiyiṉum, nummi ivviṉpa ulakattiṟkut tiṇṇamāyk koṇarntu cērppikkum eṉpataṉi yāṉ naṉku aṟivēṉātalāl, nummi varavēṟṟaṟporuṭṭu ikkoṭip pantari amippē ṉāyiṉēṉ eṉṟu moḻintāḷ. ataṉpiṉ avaḷ, cila āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ iṟantu pōy makiḻciṟanta ap pantariṉkīḻt taṉṉōṭu uṭaṉuṟintu varukiṉṟa taṉ makār iruvari avaṉiṭaṅ koṇarntu kāṭṭi, avaṉōṭu iṉṉum irukkum ēṉic ciṟumakārum iv viṉpavuṟiyuḷiṟ piṟkālattu vantu, tām ellārumāy oruṅku kūṭum vakiyāy vaḷarttu varukaveṉṟum aṟivuri kūṟiṉāḷ.
piṟaku, iṟantapiṉ tīyōr tamakku uṟiviṭamāṉa iruḷula kaṅkaḷiyum, av vamerikka tēyattuk kuṭi makkaḷiṭattuḷḷa poṟṟiraḷik kavarum poruṭṭu avar tammiṟ pallāyira vakik kattikku iriyākkiṉa nākarika millāk koṭiya airōppiyariṉ uyirkaḷ amiḻttappaṭṭuk kiṭanta poṉ ṉuruku kaṭalkaḷ palavaṟṟiyuṅ kaṇṭaṉaṉ eṉṟu ak kati mēluñ cila kūṟic celkiṉṟatu. āyiṉum, ak katiyiṉ mutaṉmiyāṉa kuṟippukaḷit toṭṭukkāṭṭi, ik kaṭṭuri yiṉ aḷavik kaṭantu ceṉṟō mākiyāl iṉṉumitaṉi viritturiyātu viṭukiṉṟōm.

5. murukavēḷ
kaṉaviṟ kaṇṭa ōviyaccāli

yāṉ veḷiyē ceṉṟu poḻutu pōkkutaṟkup pakaṟkāla nili eṉṉit taṭiceyvatāyiruntāl, eṉakku uriya naṇparil iraṇṭoruvarit terintaḻittuk koṇṭu, oru kaṭṭiṭattiṉuṭ putumiyāy vikkap paṭṭirukkum poruḷkaḷip pārkkap pōvatu vaḻakkam. ōviyaṅkaḷ allatu cittirap paṭaṅkaḷik kāṇṭalē eṉakku mutaṉmiyāṉa iṉpattit taruvatākum; pakaṟpoḻutiṟ keṭṭa ūtik kāṟṟu vīcat tuvaṅkiṉāl, nāḷ muḻutum naṭantu ceṉṟēṉuṅ kitērnta ciṟanta ōviyakkārar taṅ kikaḷāl tīṭṭiya ōviyaṅkaḷ vikkappaṭṭirukkum ōviyaccāli kāṇappōvēṉ. vāṉiṟ kariya mukilkaḷ niṟintu, nilamellām maḻipperukkil nīntuvatu pōṟ ṟōṉṟi, eṅkum mappum maṅkalumāyk karukik kāṇum poḻutu, āṟutal payavā attōṟṟaṅkaḷik kāṇum viruppa maṟṟu oḻintu, ōviyat toḻilāl tōṟṟuvikkap paṭṭa kaṟpaṉiyulakiṉi nōkkac celvēṉ; aṅṅaṉañ celvuḻi, makiḻpayavāk kār kālattil uṇṭākun tuyara uṇarvik kalittu ōṭṭik, kaḷippāṉa niṉivu kaḷāl uḷḷatti nirappum oḷi tulaṅkum nilakkāṭci kaḷiyum, miṉukkiṭṭu viḷaṅkum veṟṟit tōṟṟaṅkaḷiyum, aḻakiya mukaṅkaḷiyum, piṟaporuḷkaḷiyum av vōviyac cāliyiṟ kāṇpēṉ.
cila kiḻamikaḷukku muṉ yāṉ ittakiya kāṭci kaḷilēyē aṭuttaṭuttu īṭupaṭṭiruntamiyāl eṉ maṉak kaṟpaṉi muḻutum avaṟṟiṉ vaḻippaṭṭu niṟka, iṅṅaṉam niṉṟa niliyil oru nāṭ kāliyil yāṉ kaṇṭa oru ciṟu kaṉāviṉ tōṟṟatti muṭiyak kūṟāviṭiṉum, ataṉ mutaṉmiyāṉa kuṟippukaḷi itaṉip payilvārkku aṟivikkap pukukiṉṟēṉ.
iṭamakaṉṟu nīṇṭatōr ōviyak kūṭattiṉuḷ yāṉ nuḻintatākak kaṉavu kaṇṭēṉ; ataṉ oru pakkattil uyirōṭirukkum pukaḻpeṟṟa ōviyakkārarāl eḻutappaṭṭa ōviyaṅkaḷ niṟikkap paṭṭiruntaṉa; ataṉ maṟṟoru pakkattil iṟantupōṉa ōviyap pērāciriyar varinta ōviyaṅkaḷ niralpaṭa vikkap paṭṭiruntaṉa.
uyirōṭiruppavar pakkattiṟ palar varitalum vaṇṇan tīṭṭutaluṅ, kōlutaluñ ceytu koṇṭiruppak kaṇṭēṉ; iṟantupōṉa ōviyakkārar pakkattil, mika metuvāy varitaloṭu nirampavum nērttiyāyt toṭṭut tiruttik koṇṭirunta oruvarit tavira vēṟu evarum aṅku ōviyat toḻil purivati yāṉ kāṇakkūṭavilli.
eṉakketirē niṉṟa ōviyam vallār palariyum ārāy ntu teritaṟkut tīrmāṉittu, mutalil uyirōṭiruppavar pakkattukkut tirumpiṉēṉ. ivvōviyac cāliyiṉ ip pakkattē yāṉ mutaliṟ kaṇṭa ōviyakkāraṉ peyar taṟ perumi eṉpatām; ivaṉ taṉ talimayirip piṉṉē oru nūṉāḻiyāṟ piṇittup pirañcu tēyattu āṭavaṉippōl uṭi uṭuttiruntāṉ. āṇ, peṇ eṉṉum vēṟupāṭum akavi vēṟṟumiyuṅ karutāmal, tāṉ varinta mukaṅkaḷ ellā vaṟṟilum puṉmuṟuvalum oruvakiyāṉa nakikkuṟiyum mikac ciṟantu tōṉṟumāṟu amittāṉ. avaṉ eḻutiya naṭunili yāḷar, camayakuravar, cūḻccit tuṇivar mukaṅkaḷiluṅ kūṭa ekkaḷipput tōṉṟiyatu. curuṅkac colluṅkāl avaṉ varinta āṇpāla rellām vīṇpilukku uṭiyarāyum, peṇ pālarellām vīṇ pacappukkārikaḷāyuṅ kāṇap paṭṭaṉar. avaṉuṭiya urukkaḷiṉ mēl iṭṭirunta toṅkal āṭikaḷ anta mukaṅkaḷukkup peritum ēṟṟaṉavākavē yiruntaṉa; at toṅkalkaḷ pakaṭṭāṉa vaṇṇaṅkaḷ ellām oruṅku kuḻittu eḻutappaṭṭaṉavā yiruntaṉa; av vāṭi kaḷiṉ ovvoru pakutiyum aṅkumiṅkumāy alintu ēṉiya vaṟṟiṉun tammiyē ciṟanteṭuttuk kāṭṭa muyaṉṟaṉa.
taṟperumi eṉpavaṉukku iṭatukippakkattē pēruḻippāḷi yāṉa ōviya vēlikkāraṉ oruvaṉ niṉṟukoṇṭu, avaṉip peritum viyappōṉāy avaṉ eḻutiyavaṟṟip pārttup paṭi eḻutak kaṇṭēṉ. ivaṉ kēṭpataṟkuk kaṭumiyāṉa maṭiyaṉ eṉṉum peyaru ṭiyaṉāy yarmaṉ tēyattāṉip pōl uṭi pūṇṭiruntāṉ.
ataṉpiṉ yāṉ kaṇṭa mūṉṟām ōviyakkāraṉ peyar vīṇ eṇṇam eṉpatām. ivaṉ veṉicu tēyattuk kōmāḷipōl uṭiyiṭṭiruntāṉ. ivaṉ vīṇ kaṟpaṉi cey teḻututaliṟ kitērnta vaṉāyiruntataṉāl mukaneḷivu cuḷivukaḷiyē mikutiyum varintiṭṭāṉ. taṉatu tukilikiyāṟ ṟāṉ eḻutiya māya urukkaḷik kaṇṭu tāṉē cila nēraṅkaḷil avaṉ verukkoḷḷutalum uṇṭu. curuṅkac collu miṭattu, avaṉ varintavaṟṟuḷ mika viritteḻutap paṭṭa ōviyamāṉatu accamuṟuttukiṉṟa oru kaṉaviṉ ṟōṟṟamēyām: ivaṉ mika nērttiyāy varintiṭṭa urukkaḷum maṉattiṟkēṟṟa koṭiya vaṭivaṅkaḷē yallāmal vēṟu mēlākac collak kūṭiyaṉavā yilli eṉka.
ataṉpiṉ yāṉ terintā rāynta nāṉkām ōviyak kāraṉ mikavum virivāy eḻutuvatiṟ peyar peṟṟiruntami yāl, avaṉ piṉvaruvārkku niṉivukkuṟiyāy vittaṟ poruṭṭuk  kōliya ōviyattiṉ uruvaḻakāṉatu av vuruvukku mutalāy niṉṟōṉatu aḻaku niṟkum nāḷaḷavukūṭa nillātu virintu maṅkum vakiyāy, muṟṟum eḻuti muṭiyāmal viṭappaṭṭatu. taṉatu tukilikiyit tuppuravu ceytaṟ kēṉum, vaṇṇaṅkaḷik kalantu kuḻittaṟkēṉuṅ kālam peṟātavāṟu tāṉeṭutta vēliyi muṭittiṭa avaṉ avvaḷavu tuṭituṭit tāṉ. at tuṭituṭippukkāraṉ peyar pēravā eṉpatām.
ivaṉukku arukāmiyil, ivaṉoṭu muṟṟum vēṟu paṭṭa iyalpuṭiyavaṉum, ulāntākkāraṉ pōl uṭi yaṇintavaṉum, uḻippu eṉṉum peyaruṭiyavaṉumāṉa maṟṟōr ōviyakkāraṉum iruttalik kaṇṭēṉ. ivaṉuṭiya urukkaḷ viyakkaṟpālatām uḻippōṭum amikkap paṭṭiruntaṉa. ivaṉ ōr āṇmakaṉuruvi eḻutiṉāl avaṉ, mukattiluḷḷa oru mayiriyēṉum eḻutāmal viṭāṉ; oru marakkalattiṉ uruvit tīṭṭiṉāṟ pāymarakkayiṟṟuḷ oṉṟāyiṉum ivaṉit tappippōṉavāṟilli. avvāṟē ivaṉ cuvariṟ perumpākam eṅkun toṅkaviṭṭirunta irākkāla ōviyaṅ kaḷiṟ palaviṭattuṅ koḷutti vikkap paṭṭirunta meḻukutirikaḷāl avvōviyaṅkaḷ tāmē tammik kāṭṭuvaṉapōṟ ṟōṉṟiṉa; im meḻukutirikaḷiṉ mēṟ pakalavaṉ veḷiccañ caṭutiyil viḻāniṟpa, nāṉ ataṉi mutaṉ mutaṟ kāṇṭalum neruppu eṉṟu kattumpaṭi avi attuṇi oḷi virintu tulaṅkiṉa.
iṅṅaṉam mēleṭuttuk kūṟappaṭṭa ōviyakkārar aivarum iv vōviyaccāliyiṉ ippakkattē peritum uṉṉik kaṟ pālarā yiruntaṉar; iṉṉum vēṟu palar āṅkiruntaṉarēṉum avari uṟṟup pārkka eṉakkup poḻutu ilatāyiṟṟu. eṉṟālun tāṉē taṉṉaṟivu koṇṭu ētum eḻutā toḻiyiṉum, mika nērttiyāy muṭitta ōviyaṅkaḷi miku muyaṟciyōṭu toṭṭut tiruttik koṇṭirunta oruvaṉi aṅku yāṉ pārāmali rukkakkūṭavilli. avaṉatu eḻutu kōlāṉatu muṉṉamē niṟam mikuttu vitteḻutappaṭṭa mukattiṉ ovvōr iyaliyum mēlum mikuticeytu keṭuppatāyiṟṟu; ovvoru kuṟiyiṉiyum mēluṅ kuṟittuk kuṟivu paṭuppatāyiṟṟu; atu tāṉ toṭṭa ovvoru niṟattiṉiyuṅ keṭuttu nañcākkuvatumāyiṟṟu. uyirōṭiruppavar pakkattil iv vēlikkāraṉ ivvaḷavu tīcceyal purintaṉaṉāyiṉum, iṟantōr pakkattil ivaṉ taṉ kaṇkaḷit tiruppiyatē yilli. ivaṉ peyar aḻukkāṟu eṉappaṭuvatām.
iṅṅaṉam iv vōviyaccāliyiṉ oru pakkattip parumpaṭi yāy virintu nōkkiyapiṉ,  iṟantu pōṉa ōviyap pērāciriyar varinta ōviyaṅkaḷ niṟikkappaṭṭa maṟṟoru pakkattiṟkut tirumpiṉēṉ. tirumputalum, uṭaṉē, eṉṉip pārppavar kuḻuviṉe tirē yāṉ niṟpatākavum, āyirak kaṇakkāṉa kaṇkaḷ eṉṉi uṟṟu nōkkuvaṉavākavum eṇṇi ṉēṉ; ēṉeṉil, eṉṉetiriṟ ṟōṉṟiyavi yellām āṇkaḷ pōlavum peṇkaḷ pōlavum aṅṅaṉam uṇmi yāyt tōṉṟavē, avi ōviyaṅkaḷeṉpati ēṟakkuṟiya maṟantē pōṉēṉ. iravēl eṉpār varinta urukkaḷ oru variciyāy niṉṟaṉa; tiṣaṉ eṉpār varintaṉa maṟṟoru pakkattu niṉṟaṉa. kuviṭōrēṉi eṉpār tīṭṭiyavi piṟitorucār niṉṟaṉa.  ac cuvariṉ orupuṟattil āṉipal karācci eṉpavar eḻutiyaṉavum vēṟoru puṟattiṟ kōreṭucō eṉpavar varintaṉavum, maṟṟumoru puṟattil urūvaṉ eṉpavar varintaṉavum, niṟintiruntaṉa. curuṅkac kūṟumaḷavil, iv vōviyaccāliyiṉ ippakkatti oppaṉi ceytaṟku vēṇṭuvaṉa ceyyātu iṟantoḻinta ōviyap pērāciriyar aṅku oruvarumēyilar. ivvāciriyar palariṉ utaviyāl nili pēṟuṟṟut tōṉṟiya makkaḷ aṉivarum uyirōṭirup patu pōl uṇmiyāy viḷaṅkiṉar; avarkaḷ taṅkaḷ vaṭivat tilum, niṟattilum, uṭiyilum maṭṭun tammiloruvar oruvariṉ vēṟāka vēṟṟumip paṭṭaṉar; ivvāṟṟāl ivarkaḷ oru vakuppiṉuḷḷē palatiṟappaṭṭa makkaṭ ṭokuppiṉar pōṟ kāṇappaṭuvārāyiṉar eṉka.
iṉi, ōr ōviyattiṉiṉṟu maṟṟu ōr ōviyattiṟkup pōtalum varutalumāy, eṉakketirē niṉṟa mikavum nērtti yāṉa ellā oviyaṅkaḷiyun toṭṭut tiruttik koṇ ṭirunta mutiyōṉ (ōviyaccāliyiṉ intap pakkattil oviyavēli ceytu koṇṭiruntavaṉ eṉṟu yāṉ muṉṉarē kūṟiya orē ōr ōviyakkāraṉ ivaṉtāṉ) oruvaṉip pārt tum, avaṉ toḻil muḻumiyum yāṉ naṉṟāy uṉṉiyāma lirukkakkūṭavilli; avaṉ kiyiṟ paṟṟiyirunta tukiliki mikavut noytā yiruntamiyāl aḵteḻutu vatākavē pulaṉākavilli; āyiramuṟi toṭṭut tiruttiyum, avaṉ muyaṉṟu koṇṭirunta ōviyattiṉ kaṇ atu pulappaṭat tōṟṟuvitta toṉṟu milli. eṉṟālum, avaṉ ōvātu muyaṉṟu koṇṭirunta taṉālum, iḷippāṟu talumiṉṟi iṭiyaṟātu tirumpat tirumpat tiruttik koṇṭē yirunta taṉālum ōr uruviṉmēṟ paṭinta iṇakkamillāc ciṟu miṉu miṉuppu ovvoṉṟaṉiyum pulaṉākātavāṟu tuṭittut tūytākkiṉāṉ. iṉṉum, avaṉ niḻalōṭṭaṅkaḷukku aḻakiya paḻuppuniṟam ūṭṭiyataṉālum, vaṇṇaṅkaḷi āṟṟi mutirac ceytataṉālum ovvōr ōviyamun tammi varinta āciriyar tukilikiyiṉiṉṟu putitāy vantakkāl iruntatik kāṭṭiṉum niṟivu mikku viḷaṅkit tōṉṟiṉa. iṅṅaṉamirunta intap paḻiya ōviyaṉ mukatti yāṉ pārātu pōkak kūṭā miyāl, uṭaṉē avaṉi uṟṟu nōkki avaṉ muṉneṟṟiyiṟ ṟoṅkiya nīṇṭa kuñciyiṉāl avaṉik kālam eṉṟu teriyalāṉēṉ.
iṅṅaṉaṅ kaṇṭa eṉatu kaṉaviṉ ṟoṭarpu muṭintata ṉālō piṟitālō iṉṉateṉṟu yāṉ collak kūṭa villi. maṉattiṉ kaṟpaṉiyāyt tōṉṟiya im mutiyōṉi yāṉ neṭukap pārviyiṭṭa piṉ eṉṉi vantu cūḻnta iv vuṟakkam eṉṉi viṭṭu akaṉṟatu.

6. pēykaḷum āvikaḷum
aḻakar malikkup pakkattilē iṭintu pāḻāyk kiṭakkuṅ kōṭṭi naṭuvil āṇṭu mutirnta cilapala maraṅkaḷ niṟkiṉṟaṉa. avi mika uyarntu vaḷarntiruttaliṉāl avaṟṟiṉ ucciyil amarntirukkuṅ kākkiyuṅ aṇṭaṅ kākkiyuṅ karivatu vēṟenta viṭattiliruntō karital pōl avaṟṟiṉkīḻ vaḻi naṭantu celvārkkuk kēṭkum. iv vakiyāṉa oliyik kēṭkumpōtu, taṉṉāṟ paṭikkappaṭṭa ellāvaṟṟiṟkum vēṇṭuvaṉa vellān tantu utavuvōṉun tiruvācakac ceḻun tirumuṟiyiṟ collappaṭṭa vaṇṇan taṉṉik kūvi aḻitta karuṅkuruvikkum aruḷ purintōṉu māṉa ap perumāṉik kuṟittaḻikkum oruvakiyāṉa iyaṟki mantiravoliyē yeṉṟu yāṉ ataṉi niṉintu mika makiḻvatu vaḻakkam. pēy piṭitta iṭam eṉṟu itaṟku oru keṭṭapeyar vantamiyāl, kōyiṟ kurukkaḷi yaṉṟi vēṟu yārum iyaṅkutalillāta ivviṭatti ēṉiyavaṟṟilum mikutiyāy virumpukiṉṟēṉ. (cuṟṟuppakkattūrāṟ collap paṭṭapaṭi) mēṟkūṟiya ētuviṉālēyē kōyiṟ kurukkaḷ mārum iṟivaṉit toḻac celvāruṅkūṭa aṅku mālip poḻutu tōṉṟutalum avviṭattirāmaṟ puṟampē pōy viṭukiṉṟaṉar. itaṉāl avvaḻakar malip pakkattil evarumē taṅkuva tilli. itu paṟṟi eṉ naṇpar oruvar, ñāyiṟu maṟintapiṉ eṉṉi avviṭattiṟkut tuṇintu pōka vēṇṭām eṉṟa mikavun kuḻinta mukattoṭu taṭuttu, ōr ēvaṟkāraṉ, taliyillāta karuṅkutiri vaṭivāyt taṉ ṉetirē vanta ōr āviyāṟ kiṭṭattaṭṭat taṉṉaṟivi iḻakkumpaṭi accuṟuttappaṭṭāṉ eṉṟum, oru tiṅkaḷukku muṉ pāṟkuṭam oṉṟaṉit talimēṟ cumantu koṇṭu taṉ vīṭṭukkup pōka avvaḻiyāyp poḻutu ceṉṟu vanta paṇippeṇ orutti aṅkirunta kuṟṟuc ceṭikaḷiṉ iṭi yilē caracaraveṉṟa ōci kēṭṭu ak kuṭattik kīḻē vīḻtti viṭṭāḷ eṉṟuṅ kūṟi vaṟpuṟuttiṉār.
iraṇṭōr āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ orunāḷiravu oṉpatili runtu pattumaṇi variyil yāṉ ivviṭattē ceṉṟu ulāvik koṇṭiruntēṉ; appōtu yāṉ avviṭattiṉ nilimi yik kaṇṭu, pēy tōṉṟutaṟku mikavum ēṟṟatōr iṭam ulakattil ituvēyākum eṉṟu eṇṇalāṉēṉ. akkōṭ ṭiyiṉ iṭinta taḷavāṭaṅkaḷ aṅku ellāp pakkattuṅ kīḻum mēlumāyc citaṟik kiṭantaṉa; avaṟṟiṉmēl arippākañ cīntiṟkoṭi paṭarntu veḷḷerukku muḷittiruntaṉa; macaṅkal māliyilaṉṟip puṟattē pōtarāp palvakit taṉip paṟavikaḷum aṅkē kuṭiyāy vikiṉa; iṭintu pāḻāyk kiṭak kum paḻaṅ kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷilum, mēlmukaṭu vaḷinta avaṟṟiṉ maṇṭapaṅ kaḷilum, eppōtum naṭappataṟkumēṟ ciṟitu urakka aṭivittu naṭantu vantāl uṭaṉē etiroli tōṉṟā niṟkiṉṟatu. atē nērattil ālamara aracamaraṅkaḷ niṟkum vaḻiyiṟ ceṉṟāl am maraṅkaḷiṉ ucciyilirukkum aṇṭaṅ kākki iṭiyiṭiyē kūva avviṭam ōveṉa amiti yuṟṟup pārāṭṭappaṭat takkatāyt tōṉṟum. ipporuḷ kaḷiṉ tōṟṟamāṉatu maṉa amitiyiṉiyum āḻnta karutti ṉiyum iyaṟkiyākavē viḷivikkiṉṟatu; ivaṟṟōṭu irākkālamuñ cērntu ivviṭattiṉ veruṭciyiṉi mikutippaṭutti, aṅkuḷḷa ovvoru poruḷilum iṉṉum aḷaviṟanta tikiliṉit tōṟṟuvikkumāṉāl, maṉavalimi aṟṟavarkaḷ avviṭattiṟ pēykaḷiyum āvikaḷiyum niṟittu viṭutal paṟṟi yāṉ ciṟitum viyappaṭikiṉṟilēṉ.
ilākavar eṉṉum aṟivunūṟ pulavar niṉivukaḷiṉ kūṭṭuṟavip paṟṟit tām eḻutiya iyalil mikap putiyavāṉa aṟivurikaḷ cila kāṭṭap pukuntu, aṭuttaṭuttu nēruñ coṟpayiṟciyā luṇṭākun tappeṇṇattiṉ payaṉāy oru niṉivāṉatu, iyaṟkiyilē oṉṟōṭoṉṟu ovvāta pala niṉivukaḷi uḷḷattiṉkaṭ palakā luṅ koṇarntu oru toṭarpāyt tōṟṟuvikkiṉṟa teṉkiṉṟār. itaṟku avar eṭuttukkāṭṭiya palavaṟṟuṭ piṉvaruvatum oṉṟu: kūḷi pēy eṉṟa niṉivukaḷ eṅṅaṉam veḷiccattōṭu ētoru toṭarpum illātaṉavāyirukkiṉṟaṉavō, aṅṅaṉamē iruḷōṭun toṭarpuṭi yaṉavāy irukkiṉṟila; eṉṟālum, oru pētip peṇ oru ciṟu piḷḷiyiṉ uḷḷattil avaṟṟi aṭikkaṭi vaṟpuṟuttic colli aṅku avitammi oruṅkē eḻuppuvaḷāyiṉ, piṉ appiḷḷi periyavaṉāyt tāṉ uyirōṭirukku maṭṭum tirumpa aviyiṟṟi vēṟuvēṟākap pirikka eññāṉṟum valiyaṟṟa vaṉāvāṉ; piṟaku iruḷ tāṉ tōṉṟumpōtellām naṭuṅkac ceyyum inniṉivu kaḷiyum uṭaṉkoṇṭu varum; avikaḷ aṅṅaṉam pirippiṉṟit tōṉṟu talāl avaṉ avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟi vilakki maṟṟoṉṟit tāṅkamāṭṭu vāṉallaṉ, eṉpatām.
mālik kālattu maṅkaṟ poḻutāṉatu accatti viḷivittaṟkētuvāṉa pala nikaḻccikaḷōṭuṅ kūṭit tōṉ ṟum it taṉiyiṭattil yāṉ ulāvik koṇṭiruk kiyil, eṉakkuc ciṟitu cēymiyil ōr ā pul mēyntu koṇṭiruk kak kaṇṭēṉ; ataṉip pārttavuṭaṉ tiṭukkiṭutaṟku ici vāṉa uḷḷappāṅku uṭiyāṉukku aḵtoru taliyaṟṟa karuṅkutiriyāyt tōṉṟuvatu eḷitēyām. anta ēḻi ēvaṟkāraṉ ittakiya cila pulliya tōṟṟaṅkaḷik kaṇṭu aṟiviḻantu pōṉāṉeṉṟē yāṉ tuṇivāyc colla māṭṭuvēṉ.
eṉ naṇpar maṟṟoruvar tān tamatu kāṇiyāṭcikku mutaṉmutal urimiyāḷarāy vantapiṉ, tamatu vīṭṭil mukkāṟpaṅku muṟṟum payaṉpaṭātirukkak kaṇṭāreṉṟum, atil mikac ciṟanta ōr aṟi pēy kuṭikoṇṭateṉap peyar peṟṟataṉāṟ pūṭṭiṭappaṭṭa teṉṟum, ataṉatu nīṇṭa naṭi yiṟ kūkkuralōci kēṭpataṉāl ēvaṟkārar oruvariyum aṅku iravil eṭṭumaṇikkumēl nuḻivikkak kūṭa villi eṉṟum, atil muṉ camiyal vēli mēṟpārppavaṉ oruvaṉ tāṉ tūkkiṭṭukkoṇṭu iṟantupōṉatākat taṅ kuṭumpat tārkkuḷ oru kati vaḻaṅkivantamiyāl tammuṭiya aṟi kaḷuḷ oṉṟaṉ katavukaḷ āṇiyāl taṟiyappaṭṭiruntaṉa eṉṟum, neṭuṅkālam uyirōṭirunta tam aṉṉiyār taṅ kaṇavaṉum, oru makaṉum, oru makaḷum iṟantupōṉa aṟikaḷi arivāci aṭittu viṭṭaṉar eṉṟum mikunta nakippōṭum eṉakkup paṉmuṟi colliyirukkiṉṟaṉar. in naṇpar tamatu uṟiviṭam attuṇic ciṟṟaḷaviṉatākak kuṟukkap paṭṭataṉiyun, tamakkuriya vīṭṭiṉiṉṟum tāmum puṟam paṭuttappaṭṭa taṉiyuṅ kaṇṭu, tam aṉṉiyār uyir tuṟantavuṭaṉē ellā aṟikaḷiyum parakkat tiṟantu viṭumpaṭi kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭut, taṅ kurukkaḷmāri oruvarpiṉ oruvarāy av vaṟikaḷ ov voṉṟilum paṭukka vittiruntu pēyōṭṭuvittu, iv vāṟṟāl tam kuṭumpattārkkuḷ neṭunāḷ aracuvīṟṟirunta accam aṉittiyuṅ kalittu ōṭṭiṉār.
ittēyam eṅkaṇum nirampap paraviyiruttali yāṉ kāṇāviṭṭāṟ pakaṭi paṇṇattakka iv vaccattic cuṭṭi iṅṅaṉam ētuñ colli yirukkamāṭṭēṉ. āṉāṟ, paḻi yarum putiyaruṅ kaṭavuḷnēyam uṭiyarum uṭiyarallā rumāṉa varalāṟṟu nūlāciriyar ellārun terippaṉa vaṟṟōṭum, ellāt tēyattārun toṉṟu toṭṭu vaḻaṅkivarum varalāṟukaḷōṭum māṟukoṇṭu pēykaḷiṉ tōṟṟattip poykkaṭṭeṉṟum aṭippaṭi aṟṟa teṉṟuṅ kūṟu vārik kāṭṭiṉum, pēykaḷiyum āvikaḷiyum paṟṟi eḻūum maṉakkaṟpaṉiyāl accuṟuttap paṭukiṉṟavarkaḷ mikka aṟivuṭiyarāvareṉa yāṉ eṇṇu kiṉṟēṉ. maṉpatikaḷ kūṟum ippotuc cāṉṟurikkuyāṉ iṇaṅkak kūṭā tāyiṉum, ippōtu uyirōṭiruppavarum piṟa uṇmi nikaḻccikaḷil yāṉ nampāma liruttaṟku iṭam peṟātavarumāṉa kuṟippāḷikaḷ urippaṉavaṟṟiṟku yāṉ iṇaṅka vēṇṭiya vaṉāyirukkiṉṟēṉ. iṉṉum varalāṟṟu nūlāciriyarkaḷ maṭṭumē yaṉṟic cennāp pulavarkaḷum paṇṭik kālattu aṟivu nūlāciriyarkaḷum ikkaruttil uṭaṉpāṭu uṭiya varāka irukkiṉṟaṉar. ulukkirīciyar eṉpārun tām eḻutik koṇṭupōm aṟivārāycciyiṉ pōkkāl uṭampiṉi viṭṭu vēṟāy uyir niṟka villiyeṉa niṟuttal vēṇṭiṉā rāyiṉum, meyyākavē pēykaḷuṇ ṭeṉpatilum, makkaḷ tām iṟantapiṉ palakāl tōṉṟi yirukkiṉṟaṉar eṉpatilum aiyamaṟac colliyirukkiṉṟār; itu mikavum pārāṭṭaṟ pālateṉa yāṉ karutukiṉṟēṉ. avar tammāl maṟuttuk kūṟutaṟ kēlāvāṟu kiṭanta avvuṇmiyi vēṟoru vakiyāl viḷakki yurikkum paṭi nerukkap paṭuvārāyiṉar; aṭukkaṭukkāy niṉṟa uṭampukaḷ oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟāka iṭiviṭātu paṟantu pōkiṉṟaṉa veṉṟum, oṉṟiṉoṉṟu nuṇṇiyavāṉa iv vuṭampukaḷ kaṭpulaṉākum ip paruvuṭam pukkuṭ kaṭṭuppaṭṭirukkuṅkāṟum veṅkāyac caruku pōl oṉṟiṉuḷ oṉṟāy aṭaṅkiyirukkiṉṟaṉa veṉṟum, avi itaṉiṉiṉṟum pirinta piṉṉarc cila nēraṅkaḷil muḻumi yāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa veṉṟum, iv vētuviṉālēyē etirē yillāta allatu iṟantupōṉa māntariṉ cāyalum uruvum nām palakāṟ kāṇumpaṭi tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa veṉṟum avar nammi nōkkik kūṟiyirukkiṉṟār.
iṉi, intak kaṭṭuriyip piṟitoru katip puttakat tiliruntu īṇṭu eṭuttuc collappaṭum oru ciṟukatiyāl muṭittu viṭukiṉṟēṉ. ak katiyi iṅkeṭuttu urippatu katikkākavaṉṟi, ataṉ āciriyaṉ ataṉkaṭ potintuvitta naṉṉeṟik kuṟippukaḷōṭu vittu ataṉi muṭittuk kāṭṭu tal paṟṟiyēyām; ataṉi avar tañcoṟkaḷiṉālēyē īṇṭu varikiṉṟēṉ:
arukkaṉ eṉṉum aracaṉ putalviyāṉa kiḷimoḻi eṉpāḷ tāṉ mutal iraṇṭumuṟi maṇañ ceytukoṇṭa iraṇṭu kaṇavarum iṟanta piṉṟi mūṉṟām muṟiyum oruvaṉi maṇantukoṇṭāḷ. im mūṉṟāṅ kaṇavaṉ ivaḷiṉ mutaṟkaṇavaṉukkut tampiyāvāṉ; ivaṉ ivaḷ mītu aḷavukaṭanta viḻivu koṇṭataṉāl, ivaḷi vatuvi yayartaṟ poruṭṭut taṉ mutaṉ maṉiviyiyun turatti viṭṭāṉ. atuniṟka, ak kiḷimoḻi mikavum putumiyāṉa toru kaṉāk kaṇṭāḷ. taṉ mutaṟ kaṇavaṉ taṉṉi nōkki varuvatākavum, avaṉit tāṉ mikka urukkattōṭun taḻuvik koḷvatākavum avaḷ eṇṇiṉāḷ. avaṉik kāṇṭalum avaḷ eytiya iṉpattiṉiṭiyil, avaḷ kaṇavaṉ avaḷip piṉvarumāṟu kaṭintu kūṟuvāṉāyiṉaṉ; kiḷimoḻiyē, peṇmakkaḷ nampaṟpāla rallar eṉṉum mutu moḻiyi nī uṇmiyākkiṉiyē pār! niṉatu kumarip paruvattil niṉṉi maṇampurintukoṇṭa kaṇavaṉ yāṉ allēṉō! niṉṉāl yāṉ makkaḷip peṟṟēṉ allēṉō! iraṇṭām muṟi oru maṇamum, ataṉ piṉ, veṭkamiṉṟit taṉ ṟamiyaṉ paṭukkiyiṟ kaḷavāy nuḻinta oruvaṉi niṉ kaṇavaṉākak koṇṭu mūṉṟām muṟi oru maṇamuñ ceytukoḷḷum vaṇṇam avvaḷavukku nī namatu kātali eṅṅaṉam maṟantiṭak kūṭum? eṉṟālum, namatu paḻiya kātaliṉ ṟiṟatti nāṭi, ip paḻiyiṉiṉṟum uṉṉi viṭuvittu, uṉṉi eṉṟum emakkē uriyaḷākkik koḷkiṉṟēṉ. kiḷimoḻi ik kaṉavit taṉakkup paḻakkamāṉa makaḷir palarkkum urittup, piṉ viri vil iṟantupōṉāḷ. ikkati ivviṭattiṟku ovvātatu aṉṟeṉa eṇṇiṉēṉ; aḵtaṉṟiyum, uyirkaḷ eṉṟu muḷḷaṉavā meṉpatūum, iṟivaṉ yāvum muṉṉuṇarntu naṭattupavaṉāmeṉpatūun tiṇṇamāy meyppaṭuttik kāṭṭutaṟkuriya cāṉṟukaḷ aṭaṅkap peṟṟiruttalāl akkati īṇṭu uṟṟu nōkkaṟpālatēyām. yārēṉum iṅ kurittaṉa nampaṟpālaṉa vallaveṉṟu karutuvarāyiṉ avar tamatu karuttit tammaḷavē vittu makiḻka. ittaṉmiyavāṉa nikaḻccikaḷit teritalāl nalloḻukkattiṟ payila maṉa veḻucci tōṉṟappeṟuvāratu nampakattic citittaṟku avar muyalātirukkak kaṭavarāka.

viḷakka urik kuṟippukaḷ
- jōcap aṭicaṉ
cintaṉik kaṭṭurikaḷ eṉṉum innūlil aṭaṅkiya āṟu kaṭṭurikaḷiyum āṅkilattil eḻutiya nallicip pulavarākiya jōcap aṭicaṉ (Joseph Addison) ki.pi. 1672 ām āṇṭu mēttiṅkaḷ mutal nāḷil, iṅkilāntu tēyattut teṉ pakutiyil uḷḷa uvilṭ mākāṇattil (Wilt Shire) milṭaṉ (Milston) eṉṉum ūriṟ piṟantavar. ivartan tantiyārākiya lāṉlaṭ aṭicaṉ (Lancelot Addison) oru kiṟittu camayakuru. jōcap aṭicaṉ iḷamippoḻutilē nāṭṭup puṟattup paḷḷikkūṭaṅkaḷiṟ kalvi payiṟṟappaṭṭut tamatu patiṉintām āṇṭil ākparṭ (Oxford) nakarattil uḷḷa araciyiṉ kallūriyiṟ (Queen’s College) peruṅ kalvi payilap pukuntār. ki.pi. 1693 il, ak kalvip payiṟciyil tērccippeṟṟup perum pulamikku aṟikuṟiyāṉa paṭṭamum peṟṟār. āṅkila moḻiyiṉ maṭṭumēyaṉṟi ilattīṉ moḻiyilum ivar tuṟipōkak kaṟṟavar. kalvip payiṟciyil mikka viḻivuḷḷavar. ivar iyaṟkiyilēyē kūccam vāyntavarāyum, vāy pēcātavarāyum, ennēramum etaṉiyum āḻntu eṇṇippārkkum uḷḷam uṭiyavarāyum irun taṉar. ivar ki.pi. 1697 il, mākṭalaṉ kallūrip pulavar kūṭṭat til oruvarākat terinteṭukkappaṭṭār.
akkālattiṟ periya ōr āṅkilap pāvalarāy viḷaṅkiya ṭiriṭaṉ (Dryden) eṉpavarōṭu ivar paḻakalāṉār. araciyaṟ piriviṉaril uvik pakkattavarōṭu (Whig party) ivar cērntukoṇṭatiliruntu, appakkattavarkkut talivarāṉa cārla māṇṭēku (Charles Montague) eṉpavar kaṟṟārkku utavipuriyun taṉmiyuṭiyarākiyāl, avaratu karutti ivar tammāṭṭu īrppavarāyiṉar. accelvar, ivar pala nāṭukaḷuñ ceṉṟu kāṇum vaḻiccelaviṟku utaviyām poruṭṭu āṇṭu oṉṟiṟku munnūṟu pavuṉ allatu mūvāyira rūpā naṉ koṭiyāka aḷittu vantaṉar. kalviyiṟ ṟērnta iḷiya aṭicaṉ palanāṭukaḷuñ ceṉṟu āṅkāṅku naṭipeṟum araciyal muṟikaḷiyum, aṅkaṅkuḷḷa vevvēṟu makkaṭ piriviṉariṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷiyum uṇarappeṟutalāl tamatu pakkattavar valuveytutaṟku vaḻiyuṇṭāmeṉpatu ac cel variṉ nōkkamākum. aṭicaṉum an naṉkoṭip poruḷik koṇṭu, pirāñcu, cuviṭsarlāṇṭu, iṭṭali, jermaṉi mutalāṉa pala tēyaṅkaṭkuñ ceṉṟu, vāḻkkiyiṉ iyalpu kaḷiyum aṟivu vakikaḷiyun tērntu puttapputiya ōr aṟivuk kaḷañciyan tokuttār. ikkaḷañciyattil ivvāṟu tokuttuvitta arumperum poruḷkaḷiyē piṉṉart tām eḻutiya pāṭṭilum uriyiluñ celaviṭṭār.
mūṉṟām uvilliyam eṉṉum aracar iṟantu pōṉa vuṭaṉ, aṭicaṉ eṉṉum nampulavar peruntakiyārkku vaḻic celavukkeṉṟu aḷikkappaṭṭuvanta naṉkoṭip poruḷ niṟuttappaṭṭup pōyiṟṟu. ataṉmēl avar kīḻnāṭukaḷiṉiṉ ṟun tirumpi, 1703 cepṭamparil, tamatu tāynāṭṭiṟku vantu cērntār. appōtirunta amiccar kuḻām, pḷeṉkīm eṉṉum iṭattil ākaṭu, 1074 il tām aṭinta peru veṉṟiyic ciṟappittuppāṭa viḻintu, atu ceyyavallār oruvari nāṭukiyil, mēṟkuṟitta māṇṭēku eṉṉuñ celvar aṭicaṉē atu ceytaṟkut takkār eṉṟu kāṭṭiṉār. uṭaṉē tampāl vantu tammi vēṇṭiya avaratu vēṇṭukōḷukku iyintu ivar pāṭiya pācaṟiyiruppu (Campign) eṉṉum pāṭṭu veḷivantatu. ippāṭṭu mikac ciṟantavallāviṭiṉum, ṭiriṭaṉ (Dryden) eṉṉum pāvalar iṟantupaṭṭu vēṟu nallicic ceyyuḷ iyaṟṟuvār illā maṟ pōṉa an nērattiṟku ēṟṟatāy veḷivantamiyiṉ, aḵtu ellārāṉum peritu koṇṭāṭappaṭṭu nampulavar perun takiyārāṉa aṭicaṉukkup perumpukaḻi viḷittatu. amiccarkuḻām ataṉāṟ peritum uvappaṭintu, iraṇ ṭāyira rūpā varumpaṭiyuḷḷa ōr aluvali aṭicaṉukkut tantaṉar. ivar pāṭiya ippāṭṭil muṉṉillāta oru peruñ ciṟappu uḷatu. ivarkkumuṉ paṭittalivar kaḷip pukaḻntu pāṭiya pāvalarellārun, tām pāṭutaṟkup pukunta paṭit talivaṉ aḷavaṟṟa pōrāṇmiyu ṭiyavaṉeṉṟum, avaṉ tāṉ oruvaṉākavē taṉ oru kiyāl eṇṇiṟanta pakiñari veṭṭi vīḻttiṉaṉeṉṟum naṭavāta poyyip puṉintu kaṭṭip pāṭutaliyē toḻilākak koṇṭavarkaḷ; maṟṟu aṭicaṉē, uṇmiyāṉa muṟiyip piṉpaṟṟip, piṉ naṭakkap pōkum pōriṉ kūṟupāṭukaḷi aḷantaṟintu, avaṟṟiṟkut takkapaṭiyākat taṉatu paṭiyi vakuttamikkum āḻnta aṟiviṉ tiṟaṅkaḷiyum, etirpārāta oru pēriṭar pakiñarāṟ caṭutiyil nērntaviṭattuc ciṟitum maṉaṅkalaṅkāmal amiti yāy niṉṟu avviṭarukkut taṉatu paṭiyit tappuvikkuñ cūḻccikaḷiyun, tām pāṭutaṟku eṭuttuk koṇṭa paṭittalivaṉ uṭiyaṉataliyē ciṟanteṭuttup pāṭiṉār.
iṅṅaṉam ip pācaṟiyiruppuppāṭṭup pāṭiya kālan toṭṭu ivarkkut tirumakaḷ nōkkam naṉku vāyttatu. tuvakkattil ivar iṅkilāntiṉ utavi amiccarāyum, ayarlāntiṉ mutal amiccarāyum ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭār. poruḷ varuvāyi nirampat tarum iṉṉum pala aluval kaḷilum amarntiruntār. 1703 ām āṇṭil oru kācukūṭa illāmal vaṟumippaṭṭa ivar 1711 il, oru nūṟāyira rūpāvukku nilaṅkaḷ vāṅkiṉāreṉṟāl, iṭiyē ivar evvaḷavu poruḷ varuvāykku iṭamāṉa aluval kaḷip pārttirukkavēṇṭu meṉpati yām kūṟalvēṇṭā.
pācaṟiyiruppu eṉṉum pāṭṭiṟkuppiṉ, tām iṭṭali tēyattil vaḻicceṉṟa varalāṟukaḷip paṟṟi ivar eḻutiya nūl oṉṟu veḷivantatu. aḵtu, ivartam aḻakiyakalvit tērcciyiṉiyum, aminta melleṉṟa nakiccuvi kāṭṭun tiṟattiṉiyum, aṉpum aruḷum vāynta oḻukkattiṉiyum, ivaruṭiya nūlkaḷ ellā vaṟṟiluṅ kaṉintu tikaḻum piṭivātamillāta āḻnta camayavuṇarviṉ kiḷarcciyiṉiyum iṉitu viḷaṅkak kāṭṭiyatu.
1707 il aṭicaṉ rōsamaṇṭ (Rosamond) eṉṉum ici taḻuviya nāṭakam oṉṟi iyaṟṟi veḷiyiṭṭār. aḵtu icippāṭṭukaḷ mikaviravip payilvārkkup peritum iṉimi payappatoṉ ṟēyāyiṉum, pakaṭiyit tarum iṭaṅkaḷi laṉṟi aṭakka oṭukkamāṉa pakutikaḷil mikac ciṟantateṉṟu colvataṟku vāyātatu. iv vāṇṭilēyē ṭrammar (Drummer) eṉṉum muṭipiṉiya nāṭakam (Comedy) oṉṟum ivarāl iyaṟṟi vikkappaṭṭatu; āṉāl, aḵtu ivar iṟanta piṉṉarttāṉ ivartam āruyir naṇparāṉa ṭīl (Steele) eṉpavarāl veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu; katip pōkkilum, maṉakkavarcciyi eḻuppum vakiyilum itu kuṟipāṭuṭiya tākak kāṇappaṭiṉum, itaṉ pala pakutikaḷum nakiccuvi viḷittuk kaṟpōr uḷḷattiṟku mikunta makiḻcci taruvaṉa vāy amintirukkiṉṟaṉa.
iṉi, ivar veḷiyiṭṭa innūlkaḷiviṭa ivarkku niliyāṉa perumpukaḻittantu, āṅkilamakkaḷiyē yallāmal ulakattiṉkaṇ ivaruṭiya nūlkaḷik kaṟpā rellāriyuñ cīrtirutti, avarkaṭkellām pala tuṟi kaḷilum nallaṟi viyum iṉpattiyum ūṭṭip peritum payaṉpaṭuvaṉavāṉa urinaṭik kaṭṭurikaḷ eḻutavēṇṭum amayam ivarkku ikkālattē vantu kikūṭiṟṟu. iḷantip poḻutil oru paḷḷikkūṭattil ivarōṭu oruṅku kalvi payiṉṟa ṭīl eṉṉum pulavar, 1709 il ṭāṭlar (Tatler) eṉap periya oru putiṉattāḷi veḷiyiṭalāyiṉar. aḵtu oru kiḻamikku mūṉṟu muṟi veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu. atu veḷiyiṭap paṭṭataṉ nōkkam : akkālattu āṅkila makkaḷiṉ naṭiyuṭi eṇṇaṅkaḷic cīrtiruttutalēyām. annāṭkaḷil irunta āṅkila makkaḷiṟ celvarkaḷum naṭuttaraniliyi luḷḷāruṅ kalvic cuviyi aṟiyātavarkaḷ. avarkaḷ tamatu poḻutu pōkkiṟkāka mēṟkoḷḷuṅ koṇṭāṭṭuka ḷellām pulliyavi kaḷ; allākkāl tīya oḻukkam niṟintaṉavum vilaṅkiṉ ceykikaḷōṭu oppaṉavumām. peṇ pālāriluṅ kūṭac cūtāṭṭam mikap paraviyiruntatu. āṇpālar mēṟ koḷḷum viḷiyāṭṭukaḷō irakkamaṟṟaṉavāyk koṭun taṉmi vāyntaṉavā yiruntaṉa; avarkaḷ cārāyak kuṭiyilum maṭṭukku miñci iṟaṅki viṭṭaṉar. ittakiya nilimiyiṟ kalvit tērcciyum ataṉāl viḷiyum iṉpaṅkaḷum avarkaṭkup putumiyākavum ikaḻcciyākavum tōṉṟiṉa. ivvaḷavu cīrkēṭukaḷiyum pōkki avarkaḷic cīrpaṭuttal vēṇṭumeṉpatē ṭīl eṉṉum pulavariṉ uyarnta nōkkam. kikkum maruntu uṇṇamāṭṭārkku ataṉi uḷḷapaṭiyē vittu uṭceluttutal ākātavāṟupōl, nirampac cīr keṭṭa nilimiyiluḷḷa avarkaṭku oḻukkattiṉ mēṉmi kaḷiyuṅ kalviyiṉ ciṟappukaḷiyum nērē uṇarttap pukuntāl, avarkaḷ avaṟṟi oru ciṟituṅ kēḷār. ākavē, kikkum marunti iṉiya akkāran tīṟṟi aruttutalpōla, aḻakiya iṉiya urinaṭiyil nakiccuvi tōṉṟa ēṟkum vakiyāl eḷiya naloḻukka muṟikaḷic ciṟitu ciṟitāka nalluṇarvum iṉpamum vāyppa eḻutik kaṟpittalē ceyaṟpālatākum; itaṉi naṉkuṇarnta ṭīl eṉṉum pulavar apputiṉattāḷil eḻil kaṉinta ciṟu ciṟu kaṭṭurikaḷ varintu varalāyiṉar. tam naṇpar tōṟṟuvittu naṭattum apputiṉat tāḷi, appōtu airlāntil araciyal aluvalil amarntirunta aṭicaṉār kaṇṭavuṭaṉē, ataṟkut tāmum ariyaperiya kaṭṭurikaḷ eḻutiviṭalāṉār. ṭīl eṉṉum pulavar eḻutiya kaṭṭurikaḷip pārkkiṉum, aṭicaṉār eḻutiyavikaḷē vaṉappiṉ mikkaṉavāy ellār uḷḷaṅkaḷiyum eḷitiṟ kavarntaṉa. ṭīl eṉpavarē, aṭicaṉ urivaḷaṅkaḷ tammuṭiya urivaḷaṅkaḷiṉuñ ciṟantaṉa vātali oppukkoṇṭu, iṉittām avaratu utaviyiṉṟi ataṉi naṭāttutal iyalāteṉṟuṅ kūṟiṉār. ip putiṉattāḷ ōrāṇṭum oṉpatu tiṅkaḷum naṭipeṟṟatu. itaṉkaṇ veḷivanta mottam 271 kaṭṭurikaḷil ṭīl eṉpavarāl eḻutappaṭṭavi 188; aṭicaṉāl eḻutap paṭṭavi 42. annāṭkaḷil irunta tēnīr kāppinīr viṭuti kaḷil nikaḻntaṉa vellām ataṉkaṇ viṭāmal eḻutap paṭṭaṉa. nakarattiluḷḷa māntarkaḷiṉ vāḻkkiyiṉ iyalpu kaḷellām ataṉkaṇ naṉkeṭuttup pēcappaṭṭaṉa. iṉṉōraṉṉa nikaḻccikaḷ pulliyavikaḷē yāyiṉum, ivaṟṟiṉkaṇ uḷḷa kuṟipāṭukaḷi eṭuttuk kāṭṭi, ivaṟṟic cīrtiruttu muṟikaḷiyum patamāka iṉitu viḷakki yeḻutiṉamiyāl, ittāḷ aññāṉ ṟuḷḷār karuttit taṉ māṭṭu īrttu eṅkum pukaḻ peṟṟulaviyatu.
aññāṉṟu ṭīl eṉpavar araciyaliṟ periyatōr aluvalil amarum poruṭṭut, tām araciyalukku māṟāy ētum eḻutuvatilliyeṉṟu colluṟuti koṭuttami yāl araciyal nikaḻccikaḷiyum avvappōtu veḷiyiṭṭu vanta tamatu ṭāṭlā eṉṉum putiṉattāḷi niṟutti viṭṭu, 1711 ām āṇṭu mārccu tiṅkaḷ mutalnāḷilddd ‘Þbg¡nll®’ (Spectator) eṉṉum putiṉattāḷ maṟ ṟoṉṟit tuvaṅki veḷiyiṭalāṉār. iḵtu ilakkiyap pakutikaḷiyum, nūṟcuvi vakikaḷiyum, vāḻkkiyiṉiyalpu kaḷiyumē ciṟanteṭuttup pēciyatākum. araciyaṉ muṟikaḷ itaṉkaṇ aṟavē oḻikkap paṭṭaṉa. ipputiṉattāḷ eṅkum koṇṭāṭappaṭṭu, ellā rāṉum āvalōṭu vāṅkip pārkkappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu, āṅkila moḻiyil nikaraṟṟa nallicip pulavarāṉa milṭaṉ (Milton) eṉpavarāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭa tuṟakka iḻappu (Paradise Lost) eṉṉum arumperuṅ kāppiyattiṉ tiṭpa nuṭpaṅkaḷuñ coṟcuvi poruṭcuvikaḷum naṉku ārāynta kaṭṭurikaḷ nampulavar peruntakiyāṉa aṭicaṉāl itaṉ kaṇ oru toṭarpāka eḻutappaṭṉa. akkāppiyattiṉ viḻuppaṅka ḷiyum avaṟṟi ārāyntu teḷiyum muṟi kaḷiyum āṅkila makkaḷ appōtutāṉ mutaṉmutaluṇarntu kaḷippuṟa lāṉārkaḷ. sar rōjarṭēkavarli eṉap peyariya oruciṟu putuk katiyum mika aḻakāka ivarāl itil  eḻutappaṭṭatu. ik katiyiṉ talivaṉatu iyaṟkiyi nakiccuvitōṉṟak kāṭṭi, ataṉ vaḷarcciyiṉi neṭukappuṉintu aḻaku paṭuttum ivaratu perun tiṟaṅ, kati nūl eḻutiya vēṟuyarnta āṅkilap pulavariṭattuṅ kāṇpatu aritu. iṉṉum mirsāviṉ kāṭci (murukavēḷ kaṇṭa kāṭci) paṭippiṉ viyattaku tōṟṟaṅkaḷ pōl amitiyum urukkamuṅ kaṭavuḷ nēyamuṅ keḻumiya uyarnta aṟivurikaḷum, pakaṭiyum viḷiyāṭṭum niṟinta ārāycciyurikaḷum, pāvik kūttu, nāṭaka araṅku peṇ makkaḷiṉ naṭiyuṭi eṇṇaṅkaḷ pōlpa vaṟṟip paṟṟiya cīr tirutta vurikaḷum, viḻumiya camayak koḷkikaḷ oḻukka vakikaḷik kuṟitta meyyuṇarvurikaḷum ivarāṟ perituñ cuvikka eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. ivar toṭṭup pēcāta poruḷilli. uyarntaṉavun tāḻntaṉavum ivar kiyil vantavuṭaṉ uyarntaporuḷ mēṉmēl viḷakkam eytiyum, tāḻnta poruḷ taṉṉit tāḻttum iḻiporuṭ kalappu nīṅkit tūymiyuṟṟun tikaḻntaṉa. iṉiya karuppañ cāṟṟiṉip peytuvitta taṅkak kuḻicipōl ivareḻu tiya kaṭṭuri kaḷellām iṉimimikkut tulaṅkiṉa. ivari yaṭuttut tōṉṟiya māpperum pulavarāṉa cāmuvēl jāṉcaṉ, paḻakkattōṭu oṭṭiyatēṉum parumpaṭi yallātatum, aḻakiya tēṉum āṭamparamil lātatumāṉa ōr āṅkila urinaṭiyiṟ paḻakikkoḷḷa viḻikuvār ellārum aṭicaṉ iyaṟṟiya nūlkaḷi iravum pakalum ōyāmaṟ payilal vēṇṭum eṉa moḻintatu muḻutum poruttamēyām. eṉṟālum, aṭicaṉār iyaṟṟiya ceyyuṭkaḷiṉum, avaru ṭiya urikaḷē colaṟkarum viḻuppam vāyntaṉa vāku meṉpati ellāruṅ karuttiṟ patittal vēṇṭum. īṇṭu, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ tamiḻil moḻipeyartta kaṭṭurikaḷ āṟum aṭicaṉ pekṭēṭaril eḻutiya ciṟanta kaṭṭurikaḷi ṉiṉṟun terinteṭukkappaṭṭaṉavākum. ip putiṉattāḷil veḷiyāṉa 555 kaṭṭurikaḷil aṭicaṉāl variyappaṭṭavi 274. ittāḷiṉ 555 ilakkaṅkaḷ maṭṭum veḷivantaṉa; 1712, ṭicampart tiṅkaḷil ituvum niṉṟupōyiṟṟu.
1713 ām āṇṭil aṭicaṉ ‘nf£nlh’ (Cato) eṉap periya nāṭakakkāppiyam oṉṟaṉi iyaṟṟi veḷiyiṭṭār. aḵtu avar kālattiṟ ciṟantatākap pārāṭṭap paṭṭālum varavara ataṟku ac ciṟappuk kuṉṟiyatu. aṭicaṉāriṉ iyaṟki yaṟivāṟṟal nāṭakakkāppiyam iyaṟṟutaṟku ēṟṟataṉṟu. innāṭakattiṉ katip pōkkil iṉippu illi. katiyil varum makkaḷ marappāvikaḷiyē ottaṉar. uyirum avva varkkē yuriya ciṟappiyalpukaḷum ammakkaḷpāṟ kāṇappaṭa villi. iṉṉum ivar iyaṟṟiya urikaḷuñ ceyyuṭkaḷum pala. eṉiṉum, ivar tam perumpulamiyum perumpukaḻum eṉṟum maṅkāmal niṉṟu oḷirum maṇiviḷakkup pōlvaṉa, ivar pekṭēṭaril (Spectator) eḻutiya viḻumiya kaṭṭurikaḷēyāmeṉṟu uṇarntu koḷka.
ivar tamatu nāṟpattu nāṉkām āṇṭil, aḵtāvatu 1716 il uvārvik mākāṇac ciṟṟaraciyi maṇantu koṇ ṭaṉar. ivaḷ mutalil am mākāṇac ciṟṟaracaṉi maṇantu avaṉ iṟanta piṉ taṉiyē araca vāḻkkiyiṟ cerukkuṟṟu iruntavaḷ. ivaḷ taṉ putalvaṟkuk kalvi kaṟpikkum āciriya rāy aṭicaṉāri muṉ orukāl amarttiyatiliruntu, ivaṭkum avarkkum nēyam uṇṭāka, ataṉāl avaḷi ivar vatuvi ayarntaṉar. ēḻiyeḷiya makkaḷōṭu paḻaki avartam iyaṟki vāḻkkiyil maṉam paṟṟi niṉṟa aṭicaṉārkkut, taṟcerukku mikka araṇmaṉi vāḻkki piṭiyātāyiṟṟu. eṉavē, ivarum ivar tam maṉiviyārum maṉamotta iṉpavāḻvil vāḻntila reṉṟuṇaral vēṇṭum.
āṅkila moḻiyiṉiyum āṅkila makkaḷiyum mikac cīrtirutti mēṉilikkuk koṇarnta nallicip pulavarum, iṉiya iyalpum iṉiya ceykiyum vāyntu ullārāṉuṅ koṇṭāṭappaṭṭup perumpukaḻ mikkavarum, tammuṭiya nūlkaḷik kaṟpār ellārkkum uṇarviyum urukkat tiyum makiḻcciyiyum viḷittu avaruḷḷattip puṉitap paṭuttiyavarum āṉa aṭicaṉār 1719 il tamatu nāṟpattēḻām akaviyil mika amitiyāka im maṇṇulaka vāḻvu tuṟantār.

murukavēḷ kaṇṭakāṭci
nallici pulami malinta aṭicaṉ eṉpār āṅkilattil eḻutiya mirsāviṉ kāṭci (The Vision of Mirza) eṉṉuṅ kaṭṭuriyiṉit tamiḻil moḻipeyartturippāṉ pukunta maṟimaliṭikaḷ, avvāṅkila vuriyiluḷḷa kuṟiyīṭukaḷi aṅkuḷḷavāṟē yeṭuttut tamiḻiṉkaṇ urippiṉ, atu tamiḻc cuvikku ovvāmi kaṇṭu, avvurip poruḷi mirsā eṉpavar mēl ēṟṟāmal, avar kaṇṭa akkāṭci yit tām kaṇṭatāka vittut, tam mēl ēṟṟutaṟku muṉintu mutal aintu pakkaṅkaḷil tamatu varalāṟu kūṟukiṉṟār. itu puṉinturi vaḻakku. maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ tām iḷiñarāyi runtapōtu, tirāviṭa mantiri, nāki nīla lōciṉi, pākara ñāṉōtayam, intumatāpimāṉi mutalāṉa putiṉat tāḷkaṭkuk kaṭṭurikaḷ eḻutivarukiyil, ak kaṭṭurikaḷiṉ kīḻt tamatu iyaṟpeyarik kuṟiyāmal, murukavēḷ eṉṉum puṉivupeyariyē kuṟittu vantami yiṉ, īṇṭum aññāṉṟu nikaḻnta nikaḻcciyi ap peyar koṇṭē kuṟippiṭalāṉar.
mutal aintu pakkaṅkaḷ muṭiya maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ tām ciṟṟūril ceṉṟiruntatiyum, aṅkē nikaḻnta cila nikaḻcci kaḷiyum, avvūrp puṟakkāṭcikaḷiyum eṭutturikkiṉṟār. āṟām pakkattiṉ mutaṟ pirivu mutal avar aṭicaṉār varinta kaṭṭuriyi moḻi peyarttu, mēṟkōṭ  kuṟikaḷuḷ vitturittal kāṇka.
maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ ciṟṟūrukkuc ceṉṟa variyil avaratu varalāṟu :
1876 ām āṇṭu, cūlittiṅkaḷ 15 ām nāḷil maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ nākappaṭṭiṉattiṟ piṟantavar. ivar tam tantiyār cōḻiyac civa vēḷāḷak kulat tavirāyt tōṉṟi, nākappaṭṭiṉattiṟku iraṇṭu kal toliviluḷḷa kāṭampāṭiyiṟ peruñ celvarāy vāḻnta cokkanāta piḷḷi eṉpavarēyām; ivar tam aṉṉiyār peyar ciṉṉammi. ivar piḷḷi mip poḻutilē nākap paṭṭiṉat tiṉkaṇ irunta uveliyaṉ miṣaṉ kallūriyil (Wesley an Mission College) kalvi payiṟṟappaṭṭu varukiyil āṅkilan tamiḻ eṉṉum irumoḻikaḷiyum virumpik kaṟṟu avaṟṟil tērcci peṟṟuk kallūrit talimiyāciriyarāṟ pala paricukaḷum aḷikkappeṟṟu vantār. ivarkkup patiṉāṟām āṇṭu naṭakkiyil tamiḻmoḻi kaṟkum avā ivaratu uḷḷattil aḷavu kaṭantu eḻa, av vavāviṉi nirapputaṟ poruṭṭu, akkāliyil nākappaṭṭiṉattiṟ puttakakkaṭi vittuk koṇṭirunta iyaṟṟamiḻāciriyar tiru. ve. nārāyaṇacāmip piḷḷiyavarkaḷi yaṭintu, avarkaḷpāṟ centamiḻilakkaṇa ilakkiyaṅkaḷic cevvaṉē ōti varalā ṉār. nāḷēṟa nāḷēṟat tamiḻmoḻic cuvi ivaratu uḷḷat tip perituṅ kavarntatu. tamiḻ, āṅkilam iraṇṭilum mika uyarnta nūlkaḷik kaṟpatil ivarkku viḻivu mikuntu vantamiyiṟ, kallūriyiṟ kaṟpikkappaṭṭu vanta ciṟu nūṟ payiṟci ivaratu karuttukku iciyātāyiṟṟu. patiṉāṟām āṇṭu naṭakkiyil, tammōṭu uṭaṉpayilum māṇākkarkkut tamiḻaṟivun tamatu camayavuṇarvum perukal vēṇṭi intu matāpimāṉa caṅkam eṉap peyariya oru kaḻakam putitāka nāṭṭi ataṉi mikunta ūkkattuṭaṉ naṭatti vantār. patiṉēḻām āṇṭiṟ cavuntaravalli eṉap peyariya naṅkiyār ivarkku maṉiviyarāka maṇam poruttap paṭṭār. patiṉeṭṭām āṇṭiṟ cintāmaṇi eṉap peyariya oru peṇ makavum ivarkkup piṟantatu. ivar ciṟu piḷḷi yāyirunta kālattilēyē tantiyi iḻantamiyiṉ, tam aṉṉiyiṉ pātukāvalilēyē iruntu kalvi payiṉṟaṉar. kallūriyiṉ āciriyarkaḷum ivarōṭu uṭaṉ payiṉṟa māṇākkarkaḷum ivar taṅ kalviyaṟiviṉ tiṟattiyuṅ kūriya aṟiviyuṅ kaṇṭu mika viyantu ivarpāl mikka aṉpu pārāṭ ṭiṉar. aññāṉṟu kārikkālil naṭipeṟṟa tirāviṭa mantiri eṉṉuṅ kiḻamit tāḷukku ivar mutaṉmutaṟ cen tamiḻ kaṭṭurikaḷ eḻuti vantaṉar. ataṉpiṉ nākapaṭṭiṉatti lēyē naṭipeṟṟa nāki nīlalōcaṉi eṉṉuṅ kiḻamit tāḷukkup palap pala kaṭṭurikaḷ eḻuti vantār. ivar tamakku mutaṉ makaḷ piṟanta piṉ, kuṭumpaccumiyit tam aṉṉiyār mēl vittal iṉi ākāteṉak kaṇṭu, 1894 ām āṇṭu āṅkilak kallūrip payiṟciyi viṭṭu vilaki, ōr aluvaliṟ pukutaṟku muyaṉṟār.
akkālattil tiruvaṉantapuram aracar kallūriyil aṟivu nūlāciriyarāyirunta tiruc cuntaram piḷḷi em.ē.,  avarkaḷ, iyaṟṟiya maṉōṉmaṇiyam  eṉṉum nāṭakak kāppiyattit tām pārkka nērntatiliruntu, tam iyaṟṟamiḻā ciriyarākiya tiru. nārāyaṇacāmippiḷḷi yavarkaḷiṭañ cuntaram piḷḷi yavarkaḷ iḷantip poḻutil tamiḻk kalvi payiṉṟavarkaḷeṉpatu terintu, avarkaṭkut tammi paṟṟiyun tam āciriyarippaṟṟiyum akavaṟ pāvil oru kaṭitam varintu viṭuttār. ataṉik kaṇṭu iṟumpūtuṟṟa cuntaram piḷḷi yavarkaḷ āciriyar nārāyaṇacāmippiḷḷi yavarkaḷiyum, maṟimali yaṭikaḷiyum tiruvaṉantapurattiṟkut tam pāl varumpaṭi virumpi aḻittu oru kaṭitam pōkkiṉar. av vaḻippiṉukku mika maṉaṅkaḷittut tam āciriyarum ivarumāka 1895 ām āṇṭu navampart tiṅkaḷ kaṭikkiḻamiyil tiruvaṉantapurattiṟkuc ceṉṟu cuntarampiḷḷi yavarkaḷik kaṇṭār. ivar akavaṟpāvil eḻutiya kaṭitattik kaṇṭu  ivari āṇṭil mutiyarākak karutiyirunta cuntarampiḷḷi yavarkaḷ ivar mika iḷiñarā yiruttali nēriṟ kaṇṭa vaḷavāṉē peritum viyappuṟṟut tām muṉ eṇṇiyataṉi moḻintār. oru kiḻami variyil ivar piḷḷiyavar kaḷōṭu aḷavaḷāvi yirukkiyil ivar tolkāppiyam, pattuppāṭṭu, kalittoki mutalāṉa paḻiya caṅkat tamiḻ ilakkaṇa ilakkiyaṅkaḷiluñ, cilappatikāram, cīvakacintāmaṇi mutaliya kāppiyaṅkaḷilum vallunarāyirut tali ārāyntu pārttu ittuṇic ciṟupoḻutilē ittuṇi yuyarnta nūlkaḷi ivar ivvāṟu payiṉṟu teḷintami aritaritu! eṉap pukaṉṟu, aṅṅaṉan tām pārāṭṭiyataṟku aṭiyāḷamāka oru naṟcāṉṟu itaḻum eḻutit tantaṉar.
tirumpavum 1896 ām āṇṭil cuntarampiḷḷi yavarkaḷ ivarit tiruvaṉantapurattiṟku aḻittu appōtu mārttāṇṭaṉ tampi eṉpavarāl vittu naṭattappaṭṭa ōr āṅkilak kalvikkaḻakattil ivarit tamiḻāciriyarāka amartti vittaṉar. tiruvaṉantapura nakaram akkālattil uṭampiṉ nalam pēṇutaṟkēṟṟa oḻuṅkukaḷ amiyappeṟā tiruntamiyāl, iraṇṭarit tiṅkaḷ maṭṭumē ivar aṅkiruntu, piṉṉar avvaluvali viṭṭu nākappaṭṭiṉan tirumpiṉar. ivar tiruvaṉantapurattil irunta iraṇṭarit tiṅkaḷum, annakarattuc civarkaḷ vittu naṭattiya civa cittānta capikaḷiraṇṭiṟ civa cittānta uṇmikaḷip paṟṟiya virivurikaḷi iṭiyiṭiyē nikaḻtti vantaṉar. ivar ceyta av virivurikaḷ annakarattirunta civa naṉ makkaḷāṟ perituṅ koṇṭāṭappaṭṭaṉa. appōtu tiruvaṉantapura aracar kallūriyil nāṭakat tamiḻip paṟṟi oru virivuri ceyyumpaṭi cuntarampiḷḷi yavarkaḷ kēṭṭukkoḷḷa, ataṟkicintu 1896 ām āṇṭu cepṭampar tiṅkaḷ 12 ām nāḷ ivar nāṭakattamiḻ eṉpataṉip poruḷākak koṇṭu, mika ārāyntu kaṇṭatōr ariya virivuriyiṉi nikaḻttiṉar. avvirivuri nikaḻnta aviyil cuntarampiḷḷi yavarkaḷē avit talivarāyiruntu, ivar ceyta virivu riyiṉ arumiyi mikavum viyantu pēciṉar. av vaviyiṟ kuḻumiyirunta peruṅkūṭṭattavar ellārum ataṉip peritum pārāṭṭiṉar. appōtu ivarkku āṇṭu irupatu. avvāṇṭiṉ kaṭiciyil ivar nākikkut tirumpiṉar.
itaṟku muṉ ivar nākiyiṟ kalvi payiṉṟu varuṅkālattil, nāki veḷippāḷiyañ civacittānta capiyār, srīlasrī cōmacuntaranāyaka ravarkaḷic ceṉṉi yiṉiṉṟum iṭiyiṭiyē varuvittu avarāṟ civa cittānta arumporuḷ virivurikaḷ naṭappittu vantaṉar. nāyakaravarkaḷ nikaḻttiya avvirivurikaḷi iḷiña rāyirunta maṟimali yaṭikaḷ āvalōṭuñ ceṉṟu kēṭṭu, apporuḷkaḷ muḻumiyuṅ kēṭṭapaṭiyē maṉat tamittuc civacittānta vuṇmi teḷintu, ataṟku muṉ tām payiṉṟuvanta māyavāta vētāntam poruntāmi kaṇṭu ataṉi aṟavē ki viṭṭuc, civa cittānta vuṇmikkē uḻikkuṅ kaṭappāṭu mēṟkoṇṭu ataṉ kaṇ viruppam mītūrap peṟṟār. avvāṇṭil nākiyil naṭipeṟṟa sajjaṉappattirikā eṉṉuṅ kiḻamittāḷ oṉṟil māyāvāti oruvar, srīlasrī cōmacuntara nāyakaravarkaḷ eḻutiya cilavaṟṟi maṟuttu eḻutivantār. ammaṟuppiṉik kaṇṭa maṟimali yaṭikaḷ, nāyakaravarkaḷ eḻutiyavikaḷē poruttamuṭiyaṉa vātalum, am māyāvāti eḻutiyavi poruttamilavātalum naṉkeṭuttuk kāṭṭi, nāki nīla lōcaṉiyil toṭarpākap pala kaṭṭurikaḷ varintu, murukavēḷ eṉṟu kiccāttiṭṭu veḷiyiṭṭār. akkaṭṭurikaḷi nōkkiṉā rellām avitammi varinta ivartam ārāycci yaṟiviṉ tiṟattiyuṅ kalviyaṟiviṉ āḻattiyuṅ kaṭṭuri eḻutum āṟṟaliyum mikutiyum viyantu koṇṭāṭiṉar. ik kaṭṭurikaḷic ceṉṉiyilirunta nāyakaravarkaḷ pārkka nērnta pōtu avaṟṟiṉ tiṟatti viyantu avaṟṟi varinta murukavēḷ eṉpār yār?  eṉṟu nāki veḷippāḷiyam civa cittānta capiyārkku oru kaṭitam eḻutikkēṭṭaṉar. accapiyāruṭ perumuyaṟci yuṭiyavarum, maṟimali yaṭikaṭku iḷamitoṭṭuc ciṟanta naṇparumāṉa tiru.  maturi nāyakampiḷḷi yeṉpavar, aṭikaḷiṉ varalāṟukaḷi nāyakara varkaṭku uṭaṉē eḻutit terivittaṉar. nāyakaravarkaḷum aṭikaḷip pārkkum viruppammikkut tām aṭuttu nākikku varukiyil, ivarit tampāl aḻittu varumpaṭi avarkku aṟivittār. aṟivitta cila tiṅkaḷilellām nāyakaravarkaḷ veḷippāḷiyam vara, ivarum avaric ceṉṟu kaṇṭār. nāyakaravarkaḷ ivari yārāyntu pārttu ivarit tam putalvarpōṟ karuti aṉpu pārāṭṭi varalāṉār. nāyakaravarkaḷ ceṉṉikkut tirumpuṅ kāliyil, uṉṉi viriviṟ ceṉṉikku varuvippōm! nī antap pakkaṅ kaḷil iruntāṟṟāṉ nalamuṇṭām eṉṟu ivari nōkkik kūṟicceṉṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ aṅkē ceṉṟapiṉ ivarkku aṉpāṉa kaṭitaṅkaḷ eḻutivara, ivarum avarit tam āciriyarun tantiyumpōl eṇṇi avarpāl mikunta aṉpu pūṇṭu kaṭitaṅkaḷ eḻuti varalāṉār.
innāṭkaḷiṉ iṭiyil, nāyakaravarkaḷ ivarkkuḷḷa civacit tānta nuṭpavaṟivip payaṉpaṭuttak karutic, cīkāḻic ciṟṟampala nāṭikaḷ aruḷicceyta tukaḷaṟupōtam eṉṉum nūli ivarpāl viṭuttu, ataṟkōr uriyeḻutu māṟu tūṇṭiṉar. ataṟkicintu ivar annūliṉ nūṟu ceyyuṭkaḷukkum viḻumiyavōr aruntamiḻuri varintu ataṉi nāyakaravarkaḷpāṟ pōkka, avarkaḷ avvuriyiṉ nuṭpattiyuñ, cittāntat teḷiviyum coṟcuvi poruṭ cuvikaḷiyum uṟṟu nōkki,  ivvuri civañāṉa muṉivar uriyōṭu oppatu eṉṟu viyantupēci, ataṉit tamatu celavilēyē acciṭṭu veḷippaṭuttiṉārkaḷ. ivar akkālattē māyāvāta maṟuppāka nāki nīlalōcaṉi yil eḻutiṉa kaṭṭurikaḷiṟ cila, nāyakaravarkaḷ celavil ivar 1899 ām āṇṭu veḷiyiṭṭa cittānta ñāṉapōtam mutaṟ puttakattiṉ ku0 ām pakkam mutal ataṉ muṭivu variyil acciṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. iṉṉum akkālattē ceṉṉiyi lirunta māyāvāti oruvar, teyvat tiruvaḷḷuvar aruḷic ceyta tirukkuṟaḷiṉ mutaṟceyyuḷi māyāvātak koḷkiyiṉpāṟ paṭutti mutaṟkuṟaḷ vātam eṉap peyariya oru puttakam eḻutiviṭuppa, ivar ataṟku maṟuppāka mutaṟkuṟaḷ vāta nirākaraṇam eṉap peyar amitta nūloṉṟi veḷiyiṭṭār. ivvaḷavum ivar nāka paṭṭiṉattilirunta ñāṉṟu eḻutiyavikaḷākum. appōtivarkku āṇṭu irupatari.
iṉi, nāyakaravarkaḷ ivari nākiyiṟ kaṇṭu aḷava ḷāvic ceṉṟatumutal ivaric ceṉṉikku varuvippatiṟ karuttu mikalāṉār. aññāṉṟutāṉ, nāyakaravarkaḷpāṟ civacittānta muṇarnta tiru. nallacāmip piḷḷi eṉpavar, civañāṉapōtam eṉṉum oppuyavarvaṟṟa viḻumiya civa cittānta mutalnūli āṅkila moḻiyil moḻipeyarttu veḷippaṭuttiṉār. ataṉi veḷip paṭuttiyapiṉ, civa cittānta uṇmikaḷi naṉku paravac ceytaṟ poruṭṭu avar cittānta tīpiki allatu uṇmi viḷakkam eṉap peyar vāynta oru tiṅkaḷ itaḻit tamiḻilum āṅkilattilum naṭattak karuti, ataṉit tammōṭu uṭaṉiruntu naṭattattakkār evar eṉṟu ārāykiyil, nāyakaravarkaḷ ivarē atu ceyyavallār eṉṟu maṟimali yaṭikaḷic cuṭṭikkāṭṭi, ivarit tampāṟ ceṉṉikku varuvittaṉar. appōtu nallacāmippiḷḷi yeṉpār ciṟṟūril vaḻakkut tīrkkum maṉṟattil naṭuvarāy (Judge) aluval pārttu vantaṉar. avar nāyakaravarkaḷ kaṭṭaḷippaṭi uṭaṉē ceṉṉikku vantu ivaratu kalviyaṟiviṉiyum iyaṟki yaṟiviṉiyum tāmum ārāyntu pārttu viyantu tammōṭu ivaric ciṟṟūrkku aḻittuc ceṉṟaṉar. ivaratu utavikoṇṭu, cittānta tīpiki eṉap peyariya tiṅkaḷ itaḻ tamiḻp patippiṉ mutal ilakkam 1897 ām āṇṭu, cūṉ tiṅkaḷ, 21ām nāḷ veḷippaṭuttappaṭṭatu. ataṉ mutal aintu ilakkaṅkaḷukkē ivar āciriyarāyiruntu eḻutivantaṉar. tirumantirat tiluḷḷa aṉpuṭimi, irantārkkītal, aṟañceyāṉṟiṟam eṉṉum mūṉṟiyal muppatu pāṭṭu kaḷukkum, civañāṉa cittiyār aḷaviyiyal kāppuc ceyyuḷoṭu patiṉāṉku ceyyuṭkaḷukkum, tāyumāṉa cuvāmikaḷ pāṭal pari pūraṇāṉanta pōtam pattuc ceyyuṭkaḷukkum, poruḷ vaṇakkam eṭṭuc ceyyuṭ kaḷukkum eḻutiya virivurikaḷuṅ, kuṟiñcip pāṭṭip paṟṟi eḻutiya uriyum, aṉpu, aruḷ eṉpavaṟṟip poruḷākak koṇṭa kaṭṭurikaḷum āṅkilattiṉiṉṟu moḻipeyarttu varinta nāṉku ceyyuṭkaḷum mūṉṟu kaṉavu eṉpatum, vēṟu cila kuṟippu kaḷum appōtu ivarāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭu, ac cittānta tīpiki mutal mūṉṟilakkaṅ kaḷil veḷippaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa vākum. mēṟkuṟitta nūlkaḷukku ivareḻutiya virivurikaḷum, maṟṟik kaṭṭurikaḷum, ivar puttiḷamik kālattilēyē eytiya arum peṟaṟ kalvip pulamiyiṉiyum āḻnta aṟiviṉ ṟiṟattiyum viḷaṅkak kāṭṭum pēraṭiyāḷaṅkaḷāy niṟkiṉṟaṉa. ivvaḷavum ivar ciṟṟūrkkuc ceṉṟavariyil nikaḻnata varalāṟām. ittuṇiyē innūliṉ urik kuṟippukaḷukku vēṇṭu vatākiyāl, mēl nikaḻnta ivaratu varalāṟṟiṉi īṇṭu kūṟātu viṭukiṉṟām.
ivar ciṟṟūriṟ ceṉṟiruntapōtu ivarkku āṇṭu irupatu nirampi oṉpatu tiṅkaḷ mēl āyiṉa. ākiyāṟ kaṭṭuriyil irupatāṇ ṭeṉṟatu oru kuttu matippē yām.

cintaṉik kaṭṭurikaḷ
colviḷakkam
1. murukavēḷ kaṇṭakāṭci
nekiḻap peṟāta - viṭṭunīṅkap peṟāta,  iṅkē celvar eṉṟatu civañāṉa pōtatti āṅkilattil moḻipeyartta nallacāmippiḷḷi yeṉpāri, illam - vīṭu, oruṅku iruntu - oṉṟu cērntiruntu.
aḷavaḷāy - maṉaṅkalantu, viyantu - pārāṭṭi, viḻivu - mikunta viruppam; naṭpu - nēcam; keḻu takimi - urimi  viḻuppam - ciṟappu;  eytum - aṭiyum; pēṟu - celvam; piṟitoṉṟu - maṟṟoṉṟu; uṇṭukol - uṇṭō; pularikkāli - viṭiyaṟkālam.
miḻaṟṟal - pāṭutal; aravam - oli; vikaṟi - viṭiyaṟ kālam; ippakkam muṟṟiluñ ciṟṟūrkku veḷiyēyuḷḷa nila amippiṉ aḻaku  aṅkuḷḷapaṭiyē eṭuttuc collappaṭu kiṉṟatu, uḷḷavaṟṟi uḷḷapaṭiyeṭuttu aḻakupaṭuttik colvatē nallici pulamiyākum, ōriṭatti aḻakupayappac colluṅkāl aṅkuḷḷa uṇmikku māṟākattam maṉam pōṉapaṭi yellām eḻutum piṟkālattut tamiḻp pulavariṉ ceyyuḷ vaḻakkuc ciṟitum virumpat takkataṉṟu. pattup pāṭṭu kalittoki puṟanāṉūṟu   mutalāṉa paḻiya caṅkat tamiḻ nūlkaḷiṟ pōnta pāṭṭukaḷellām uḷḷavaṟṟi uḷḷavāṟē eṭuttup pāṭum viḻuppam vāyntaṉavāyiruttal peritum pārāṭṭaṟpālatākum.
puṟampē - veḷiyē;   maṉaveḻucci - uḷḷakkiḷarcci; aṭarnta - neruṅkiṉa; tōppu eṉpatu orēvakiyāṉa periyamaraṅkaḷ aṭarntiruppatu; kūṭṭam eṉap poruḷpaṭum; tokuppu eṉuñ col tōppu eṉṟu āyiṟṟu;  veṭṭaveḷi - naṭuvē  maṟippāka ētoru poruḷum iṉṟi veṟumparappā yiruppatu; veṭṭa, veḷi eṉṉum iraṇṭu coṟkaḷum veḷ eṉṉum mataṉiliyiṟ piṟantaṉavākum; kāṉyāṟu - kāṭṭāṟu.  āṇṭu āṇṭu - avvavviṭattē; muruṭu eṉṉuñ col ik kālattē muraṭu  eṉa vaḻaṅku kiṉṟatu; vēṉiṟ kālam - veyiṟkālam,  atu cittirimutal āṭit tiṅkaḷ īṟākanikaḻvatu.  akaḻnta - tōṇṭiya.
pūṭṭi - irāṭṭiṉam; payakkum - uṇṭākkum, āruyir - uṭampil niṟinta uyir; ariya uyir eṉalum ām. puṭkaḷ - paṟavikaḷ. nākaṇavāyppaṟavi ikkālattār nārattampiḷḷi eṉavum miṉā eṉavuṅ kūṟuvar. 
tokuti - kūṭṭam, nāyuruvip pūṇṭiṉ vēr pal tulakkuvataṟku mikavum ēṟṟateṉpar.  tiraḷe - tiraṇṭa poruḷ.  potintu - tiṇintu: uḷḷaṭakki; aṟivuṟuttal aṟiviṟpatittal.
ulaka iyaṟkiyiluḷḷa nalaṅḷi mutaliṟ kaṇṇi ṉāṟkaṇṭu piṟaku aṟiviṉāl eṇṇip pārāṭṭal vēṇṭum. kaṇṇiṉāṟ kāṇpataṟkō pakalavaṉatu oḷi kaṭṭāyam vēṇṭiyirukkiṉṟatu. oḷiyit taruvatōṭu, nilattiṉkaṇ uḷḷa pul pūṇṭukaḷ marañceṭi koṭikaḷ, paṟavikaḷ, vilaṅkukaḷ, makkaḷ eṉṉum ellā uyirkaḷiṉ vaḷarccikkum avaṟṟiṉ aḻakiya niṟaṅ kaḷukkum appakalavaṉ veḷiccamē ētuvāyirukkiṉṟatu. eṉavē, ulakattiṉkaṭ kāṇappaṭum ellā nalaṅkaḷum ñāyiṟṟiliruntē uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa. ātalāl , akaṉṟu elliyaṟiyappaṭāta ulakiṉ nalaṅkaḷ ellāvaṟṟiyum oruṅkē orukālattu uṇaramāṭṭāta ciṟṟaṟiviṉarāya makkaḷ, orukālattu oruṅku kāṇappaṭum ñāyiṟṟiṉik kaṇṭa vaḷavāṉē ataṉ kaṇ avvellā nalaṅkaḷiyum eḷitil uṇarap peṟukiṉṟaṉar. ivvuṇmiyiṉit terikkum poruṭṭē ñāyiṟṟiṉukku oru poṟkuṭatti uvamiyākki, ulaka viyaṟkiyiṉ nalaṅkaḷi akkuṭattiṉuṭ peyta paṇṭaṅkaḷākki urittār.
kavintu  - mūṭi. maḻukki - maṅkac ceytu. kaṇṇaṟivu nikaḻap peṟātārkku maṉavaṟivu mikak kuṟukutal kuruṭar māṭṭuk kaṇṭu koḷka. iruḷ mika virinta parappu uṭiyatāyiṉum, ōriṭattuc ciṟitāyt tōṉṟum oru viḷakkoḷiyāl atu maṟintoḻitalukku, aṟivoḷiyāl maṉa iruḷ māytali uvami kāṭṭiṉār. iruḷ karuniṟattatākaliṉ ataṉip pōkkum ñāyiṟṟiṉi veṇṇiṟak katirkaḷeṉṉun tīmpāl peyta oru poṟkuṭamāka uruvakappaṭuttiṉār; mēlum, appoṟkuṭam, innila vulakattup poṟkuṭampōl taṉṉuḷ peytataṉi maṟikkum iyalpiṟ ṟaṉṟi, maṟṟup paḷiṅkuk kuṭam pōṟ ṟaṉṉuṭ potintataṉi veḷiyē teḷivuṟak kāṭṭum iyalpiṟṟōṉṟum, nāṟpuṟattum pāyum ataṉ veḷḷiya katirkaḷ akkuṭattiṉ eppakkattun tōṉṟum pāloḷiyi ṉoḻukkup pōlvaṉavā meṉṟuṅ kuṟippiṭṭār. aṅṅaṉam pāyuṅ katirkaḷ nīṇṭa kampi vaṭivattāṉum iruḷic citikkun toḻilāṉum, yāṉi, kalyāṉi mutaliya vilaṅkiṉaṅkaḷ mēṟ pāyum ampukaḷ pōṟaliṉ, piṉṉar avaṟṟik kaṇikaḷākavum uruvakap paṭuttiṉār. ataṉpiṟ, puṟattē tōṉṟum ñāyiṟu puṟattiruḷi nīkkik kaṇṇiviḷakki, ammukattāl akattē paraviya iruḷiyum pōkki maṉavaṟivi viḷakkutal kuṟittār. elli kāṇappaṭuñ ciṟuvaṭiviṟ ṟākiya ñāyiṟē puṟaviruḷ akaviruḷ iraṇṭiyum pōkkip, puṟakkaṇ akakkaṇ iraṇṭiyum viḷakkavallatāyiṉ, elli kāṇappaṭāta iṟivaṉatu aruḷoḷi nam uḷḷattil eḻuṅkāl aḵtu aṅkuḷḷa aṟiyāmiyi muṟṟum nīkku meṉpatuṅ kāṭṭiṉār.
amiḻtam-pāl.  katirkaḷ - oḷikkampikaḷ. kaṇikaḷ - ampukaḷ.  kaṭpulaṉ - kaṇ aṟivu.
viyattakum - pāraṭṭattakum. aḷappu aritu - aḷantu collutal aritu. cūṟi - cuḻalkāṟṟu. tiripu - nilimāṟal. iyakkam - naṭamāṭṭam. makkaḷ vāḻkkiyiṉ niliyāmikku vāṉ niḻalum, uyir uṭampil uyarattil ulavum vāṉam ōyāmaṟ kāṟṟāl untappaṭṭu ōṭikkoṇṭē yiruttaliṉ, kīḻē nilattiṉ mēṟ paṭum ataṉ niḻalum ōriṭattum nilipeṟāmal ōṭikkoṇṭēyiruk kiṉṟatu. atupōlavē makkaḷ vāḻkkiyum nili peṟāmal virintu ceṉṟu maṟintu pōkiṉṟatu. iṉi, nēṟṟuk kaṇṭa kaṉavu kaṇṭa appoḻutē maṟintu pōtal pōlavum, atu tirumpattōṉṟi iṉṟu kāṇum kaṉavoṭu toṭarpu paṭṭu nikaḻāmi pōlavum, oru nērattil ōruṭampil niṉṟa uyir, niṉṟa an nērattilēyē ataṉi viṭṭup pōtalun, tirumpa ataṉ kaṇ vantu pukuntu toṭarpuṟa nillāmiyum kaṇṭu koḷka; ippakuti mutal nūliliruntē moḻi peyart turikkappaṭṭatākum.
āvatu varimutal aṭicaṉ eḻutiya kaṭṭuri nērē moḻi peyartturikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. malikkuvaṭu - maliyucci. āṭi - pōrvi.  naṇpakal - naṭuppakal, naḷ eṉpatu naṇ eṉat tirintatu.  iḷamarakkā - muṟṟā maraṅkaḷ uḷḷa cōli. vēy - mūṅkil. cilir cilirppu - mayirkkūc ceṟital; pullāṅkuḻal ici kēṭkum iṉpattāl mayir cilirkkum. kaṉṉaṟpāku - karumpiṉ cāṟu. immippiṟavi - ippōtuḷḷa piṟappu. nukarutal - uṇṇal.  tuṟakkam - mēl uḷḷa iṉpa ulakam. tuytta - nukarnta.  arampimātar - tuṟakkavulakattil uḷḷa aḻakiya mātar.  eḻil - aḻaku.
icik karuvikaḷāṉa : yāḻ kuḻal tāḷam muḻavu mutaliyaṉa.  tīviya - iṉiya, ippiṟaviyilirunta kālattu nalviṉi tīviṉikaḷic ceytōr, iv vuṭampi viṭṭapiṉṉum āvi vaṭivil innila vulakattiṟ karukē iyaṅkutaluṇṭu. itu maraṇattiṉ piṉ maṉitar nili eṉṉum nūliṟ kaṇṭu koḷka. uriyāṭiṉāl - pēciṉāl. icitta - olippitta, muṟuvalittu - puṉcirippukoṇṭu. nal uri - naṟcol; kūṟi - colli.  veruṭṭi - veruḷac ceytu, turatti.
kīḻppāl - kiḻakkuttici. paḷḷattākku - malikaḷiṉ iṭiyē amintirukkuṅ kuḻinta nilam. oḻukuvatu - ōṭuvatu. makkaḷ vāḻkkiyiṉ iyalpukaḷi appaḷḷattākkiṟ kāṇappaṭum kāṭcikaḷōṭu porunta vittuk kāṭṭukiṉṟār. tuṉpavēli - tuṉpaṅkaḷukku iruppiṭam. makkaḷ vāḻkkiyil varuntuṉpaṅkaḷ oru perumpaḷḷattākkākavum, attuṉpaṅkaḷ ellām nēṟṟu iṉṟu nāḷi eṉṉuṅ kāla ōṭṭattippaṟṟi nikaḻtaliṉ akkāla ōṭṭam appaḷḷat tākkiṉūṭu curuṇṭu ōṭum oru nīrpperukkākavum, ulakattiṉ toṭakkamum ataṉ muṭivum evarāṉum aṟiyappaṭāmiyiṉ, avviraṇṭum annīrp perukkil iruṇṭu iṭiyē tōṉṟiya nammakkaṭ piṟaviyiṉ vāḻnāḷ annīrp perukkiṉ naṭuvē kāṇappaṭum oru pālamākavum, makkaḷ vāḻkkikku variyaṟukkappaṭṭa nūṟu āṇṭukaḷum ap pālattil aminta nūṟu kaṇ kaḷākavum, avaravar tan takāc ceyalkaḷāl nūṟāṇṭu niṟitaṟku muṉ eḻupatāṇṭukkuḷ iṟantu pōtaliṉ, maṟṟi muppatāṇṭukaḷum appālattil uṭintupōṉa kaṇ kaḷākavum uruvakappaṭuttic collappaṭṭaṉa.  tiṇinta - neruṅkiya. ētu - iyipu, kāraṇam : ētilār eṉṉum paḻiya tamiḻc col iyipu illār eṉap poruḷ tarutaliṉ itaṉi vaṭacol leṉṟal poruntātu; ētu eṉṉun tamiḻc collē hētu eṉat tirittu vaṭanūlā rāl vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. viṉaviṉēṉ - kēṭṭēṉ. variyaṟukkap paṭum - elli kaṭṭappaṭum: pakuti - paṅku. vāṇāḷ - vāḻnāḷ. nōkkutal - uṉṉippāy pārttal. nuṟuṅkutal - noṟuṅkutal; poṭi paṭutal. mottam - toki.
iṟivaṉ mutaṉmutal makkaḷip paṭitta kāliyil avarkku āyiram āṇṭu vāḻnāḷ amittāṉeṉpatu viviliya nūl vaḻakku. vāḻnāḷ eṉkiṉṟa pālattiṉ iṭiyiṭiyē poruttappaṭṭa kaḷḷak katavukaḷāka uruvakap paṭuttappaṭṭavi, makkaḷ tam poruntāc ceyalkaḷāl varuvittuk koḷḷum nōykaḷēyākum. kālam mutirā muṉṉarē iṟantoḻivataṟku innōykaḷē ētuvāka iruttalāl, makkaḷ caṭutiyiṟ ṟavaṟikkīḻē veḷḷattil vīḻtaṟku vāyilāy appālattil amikkap paṭṭa kaḷḷakkatavukaḷāka ivi tammi uruvakappaṭuttiṉār. appālattiṉ mutaṉ muṉiyil toṅkuṅ kariya vāṉam vāḻnāḷ tuvakkattiṟku ōr aṭiyāḷamākaliṉ, ataṉik kuḻantip poḻutilē yaṇukiya makkaḷ, tāy tantiyar vaḻivanta palvaki nōykaḷāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭu iṟantoḻi talit terittaṟ poruṭṭu aṅkē ak kaḷḷappaṭukuḻikaḷ paṟpala uḷa eṉṟār.  nuḻivāyil - uṭpukum iṭam.  pālattiṉ naṭu veṉṟatu makkaḷ vāḻnāḷiṉ naṭuppakuti. atu muppattintu mutal nāṟpattintu āṇṭu vari yiluḷḷa kālam; akkālattiṟ perumpālār iṟantu paṭutaliṉ, avviṭattiṟ cellac cella makkaḷiṉ toki curuṅki viṭṭateṉṟār.
appālattiṟ paḻutupaṭātirunta kaṇkaḷ eṉṟaṉa, nōyillāta iḷamikkālamēyām: ak kālattil makkaḷ toki perukiyirut taliṉ muḻumiyāyirunta kaṇkaḷiṉ mēl avaratu toki mikunta teṉṟār.
paḻutupaṭṭa kaṇkaḷeṉṟaṉa nōyāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭa kālaṅkaḷ; akkālaṅkaḷiṟ ciṟitu uyir piḻittiruntōrum nāṭ cellac cella iṟantu paṭṭami aṟivittār. teṟṟup paṭṭu - taṭippaṭṭu.  nīrkkumiḻikaḷ eṉṟaṉa palvakic ciṟṟiṉpaṅkaḷ.  koṭuvāḷum arivāḷum ēnti niṉṟu, vaḻivan tōri appaṭukuḻikaḷiṟ pukutti vīḻttiṉōr, kaḷavu, kaṭkuṭi, cūtu, ūṉuṇā, vilimātar.
